- 0.1.159 (2026-11-01): the Giant Yeti, the first boss (module 10; Austin: "then lets go to the next module"; design research/boss_plan.md). Wild one zoocraft:wild_giant_yeti (400 health = its energy, family zoocraft_boss, a 27-cause damage sensor, knockback resistance 1, persistent, home radius 10; groups calm/seek_den (move_to_block moss_carpet)/rest/chase/chase_angry (nearest_attackable_target + melee_box_attack with attack 0)/hold, and boss_bar (minecraft:boss, hud 48) toggled once per fight), zoo one zoocraft:giant_yeti (calm, the zoo groups), the snowball zoocraft:yeti_snowball (projectile, impact damage {min 5, max 5}, no anchor and no semi_random_diff_damage, as vanilla 1.26.50's snowball: the bench showed 1.26.50 refuses the whole entity for a number as either, or for that member), the Yeti Cage (items yeti_cage / yeti_cage_occupied, recipe BIB/BCB/BIB with iron bars, packed ice and a Reinforced Crate at the Zoo Workshop), Yeti Tracking research, the art from 0.1.159b (model 3.6 blocks, skin, animations, three particles). Scripts animals/bossRules.js (the rules: the energy floor in the before-hurt event (health already less the hit: before = current + damage), provoking, the moves, who they hit, the snowball's aim, lairs, the zoo day, the snow measure), animals/boss.js (the fight, every 2 ticks: roar 40 ticks, a move every 50 (30 angry) with a wind-up, the swipe and slam by applyDamage + applyKnockback inside an `applying` flag so the engine's melee hits are cancelled, the hurl by spawnEntity + shoot; angry at 200: Slowness II 3 s and a push within 12; tired at 40; up rested after 1200 ticks with nobody within 32, kept across loads by a real-time stamp (Date.now(), bossRules.restedSince: the world's absolute time stood still at 1000 on the bench with the daylight cycle off, so a yeti left alone never got up); the fight ends with nobody it fights in the ring of 32 round its home or a minute with no hit; lairs every 20 ticks: a park member in a taiga-tagged biome with no lair within 800 gets one 32 to 48 off (inside the 4 ticking chunks), level, natural ground, 160 from parks, built as a job and marked built at the end (a half-built one is built again, 3 tries), the yeti put in its den; a missing yeti put back after 3 looks), animals/yeti.js (the zoo day: feeder, toss, roar, sit, sleep at night). Wiring: species zoocraft:giant_yeti (boss, cold, snowFloor 0.5, cover 6%, favourite, Fruit and Fish Rations, 1 to 2, no young), profile yeti 400/200/5, the snow measure (exhibits.js, ex.snow; comfort capped at 50 without it), the carrier gate (carriers.js: bossCatchBlock before, bossCaught after: the lair turns caught), Places yeti_lair with the way to the nearest wild lair (places.js lairPlaces), journal tasks yetiroar/yetihurl/yetitired, the clipboard and journal lines, the test zoo's yeti exhibit (26 x 25, snow, a den, a pair). Bench (BDS 1.26.60, Easy, bdstools/yb_bp and dbgboss159.py; worlds yf1-yf4, yf3b and yl1-yl2): Creative or sneaking 3 off: no fight; walking up 4 off: roar, bar on, swipes 4.5 (pushed 2.7), slams 4 (2 up, 3.2 back), snowballs hitting for 5 from 7 to 20 off; angry at 186 with Slowness II; 50 -> 42 -> cut 2 -> 40 tired, bar off, later hits nothing, the cage check ok only then; rested at 400 after a minute with the player 40 off, and when met again after a minute away (still tired after half a minute); the fight ended walking off; no engine melee hit got through. Lairs in a taiga (seed 12345, 864 -1632): made 31 and 44 blocks off and built, no second one, a removed yeti put back, at night asleep on its moss bed, up in the morning, it walks out of its den, the fight in the lair ends past the ring. 321 tests (three new). How: gen159/yeti159.py (art), ents159.py, bossRules.js, boss.js, yeti.js, wire159.py, tests159.py, docs159.py, ship159.py. A full Minecraft restart. Test plan: docs/Giant_Yeti_test_plan.md. - 0.1.160 (2026-11-01): Lava Rays you can see in the Lava Falls, and the Lava Strider Book (Austin: "I am not seeing any lava rays in the lava falls biome, lets also add a lava strider enchantment for boots that allows for normal speed travel in lava"). Why no rays: the game puts a spawn only in lava within 44 blocks of a player (a 3D sphere), and on the Lava Falls land that lava is mostly the springs set into the roof and walls (0.1.155) and falling streams; the bench (seed 12345, a player on the land at 400 83 50, 6 minutes) got 2 rays, one in a roof spring at y 111 and one in a wall block at y 80; held over the sea, 1 in 6 minutes. Fix: animals/lavaRays.js (loop "lavarays" every 40 ticks, each player every 200): in the Lava Falls, wild rays within 48 that aren't in open lava (lavaRayRules.inOpenLava: a lava column topped by air with lava on 3 or more of the 8 cells round its top) are moved to open lava within 16 or removed (one within 12 of a player is left alone; the game's own spawns are looked at 2 ticks after entitySpawn); with fewer than 4 near and doMobSpawning on, 1 or 2 are spawned in open lava (5 of 8 round it) 8 to 26 out and within 30 (despawn_from_distance starts at 32), 48 columns a look, Lava Falls biome at the spot. The look down is getBlockBelow from the air at the player's feet + 1 and + 6; it passes through lava even with includeLiquidBlocks (the bench: 400 looks over the land, 281 open lava tops near, 0 stops on lava), so lavaOverFloor follows the lava up from the floor it stops on. Bench after: a player standing on the land at 400 83 50 got 4 rays put in pools 12 to 26 blocks off in the first minute, and 4 were near 3 minutes in (2 in their pools, 2 out on the bank by them); at 365 85 85, with fewer pools, 7 put over 3 minutes and 4 near at the end; 4 rays put into springs in the rock 14 to 30 off: 2 moved at once to pools 14 blocks away, the 2 with no pool within 16 taken away. The game's own spawns go on too. Lava Strider: equipment/striderRules.js + lavaStrider.js; the item zoocraft:lava_strider_book (glint, uncommon, stack 1; recipe MFM/FBF/MFM magma cream, warped fungus, book, at the crafting table and the Zoo Workshop) used with boots on adds the lore line "§r§7Lava Strider" (setLore, their lines kept; an item dynamic property set by the book read back unset on the worn boots on the bench, so the line carries it); loop "strider" every 10 ticks sets the player's minecraft:lava_movement attribute (every player has it, default 0.02) to 0.11 with such boots on and resets it to the default with them off. Bench (bdstools/lsb_bp, a lava lane 2 deep): ground 4.27 b/s, lava 0.78, Speed III in lava 0.78, lava_movement 0.1 -> 3.90, 0.3 -> 11.64, the book's boots 4.28, off 0.78, the line kept through unequip and re-equip, the book used up in Survival. Enchantments of an add-on's own aren't possible in Bedrock, so it is a book. 324 tests (three new). How: gen160/lavaRayRules.js, lavaRays.js, striderRules.js, lavaStrider.js, strider160.py, wire160.py, tests160.py, docs160.py, ship160.py. A full Minecraft restart (a new item and texture). Test plan: docs/Lava_Rays_and_Lava_Strider_test_plan.md. Next: Soul Sloth 0.1.161, Baby Dragon 0.1.162.
- 0.1.161 (2026-11-01): the Soul Sloth, the second boss (module 10; Austin: "then lets go to the next module"; design research/boss_plan.md). Wild one zoocraft:wild_soul_sloth (400 health = its energy, family zoocraft_boss, the yeti's damage sensor, knockback resistance 1, persistent, home radius 8, fire immune, box 1.9 x 2.4, int property zoocraft:act 0-15 synced to the client); `minecraft:physics` is in every movement group and never in the base (the bench, 0.1.160: with it in the base, removing a has_gravity false group leaves it floating), the hang group's physics has_gravity false. animals/boss.js now plays two kinds (KINDS: the yeti and the sloth; BOSS_TYPES in bossRules.js is a literal list, as slothRules imports bossRules): the sloth's calm is a hang cycle on its own clock (asleep 1600-3600 ticks, awake 400-1000, down after an awake spell 35% of the time for 500-1200 ticks), moved by script (animals/slothMoves.js: glides a step a tick by teleport, climbing 0.075 blocks a tick, the turn onto the beam 16 ticks, walking 0.07); provoked as the yeti (a hit, or a Survival or Adventure player within 5 not sneaking), up on its beam it drops (30 ticks at most) and howls (44 ticks). Moves (animals/slothRules.js SLOTH): rake 7 (reach 5, arc 70, strike at 19 of 30), soul wail 4 all round within 7 (Slowness I 60 ticks, strike at 24 of 40), soul clump lob 7 to 22 off (zoocraft:soul_clump, power 1.0, gravity 0.05, 5 on a hit, a 6 s timer); angry at 200 (Slowness II 60 ticks within 12), tired at 40, rested after 1200 ticks with nobody within 32 (the yeti's Date.now() stamp). Lairs (trenchRules sloth_lair, slothRules SLOTH_LAIR): in minecraft:soulsand_valley, 800 apart, 32 to 48 from the player, the ground found going down from 12 over the player to 24 under (the valleys are terraces under a roof; netherGroundY, slothSiteLevel), 9 air over the clearing, level within 3; a clearing of radius 8 of soul soil (soul sand patches), the fossil arch (bone block pillars 3 out from the middle, the beam 7 up, pillar_axis set), bone brackets with hanging soul lanterns, soul torches, 4 basalt columns, bone ribs and loose bones; the sloth's spot hangSite: 1.92 + 0.5 out from the post's face, hangY = beam - 2.53 + 0.1, climbY = beam - 4.202 - 0.05 (the model's measures, SLOTH_BODY). The zoo one zoocraft:soul_sloth (moving/still/hanging groups, a feeder seek, homes; can't be hurt) and animals/sloth.js (loop "sloths" every 10 ticks, "slothmove" every tick): its exhibit's beams from measureSloth (a run of 5 solid cells in a row 5 to 8 over the floor with a solid post at its back end reaching the floor, up to 4 kept), one per sloth, hangs, sleeps, wakes, climbs down to eat, rake, yawn, sit, and back up; no beam, it sleeps curled up. Exhibit rules (profile sloth 400/200, headroom 5; balance soulComfortCap 50, beamComfortCap 60; species soulFloor share 0.5, beam, heat): the Exhibit Clipboard's "Soul underfoot" and "A beam to hang from" lines and care warnings. The Soul Cage (zoocraft:soul_cage, recipe BLB/SCS/BLB iron bars, soul lantern, soul sand, Reinforced Crate at the Zoo Workshop; Soul Tracking research needs 5 ready, 2 gold blocks, 8 soul sand, a compass, 300); Field Journal tasks slothhang, slothlob, slothtired; Places "Soul Sloth Lairs". The test zoo gets a sloth exhibit (26 x 25, soul soil with a soul sand patch, two bone posts with beams 7 up) and grows to 220 x 220 (58 exhibits). Bench (BDS 1.26.60, Easy, seed 12345, Nether 440 440; bdstools/slb_bp, sloth161.sh and runs C to E): lair built 36 off in 0.19 s (230 floor blocks, 38 bone, 13 basalt, 2 lanterns, 2 torches), hung at 89.57 under the beam at 92; the calm cycle twice round; Creative and sneaking 3 under it: asleep; walking up: dropped in 0.6 s, howl 2.1 s, bar on; rakes 4.5, wails 3, clumps 4 of 5 hit for 5 from 12; angry at 194 with Slowness II about 3 s and a push 3 -> 5.3; tired at 40, the bar off, hits nothing, the cage check no then yes; the player 168 off 70 s: met again at 400, walked home, climbed, hung asleep; the test zoo: both sloths each on its own beam (sites 9.92 and 22.08 at -56.43), the cycle round, 150 s, no errors. 326 tests (two new). - 0.1.162 (2026-11-01): the Baby Dragon, the third boss (module 10; Austin: "then lets go to the next module"; design research/boss_plan.md). Wild one zoocraft:wild_baby_dragon (300 health = its energy, family zoocraft_boss, the bosses' damage sensor, knockback resistance 1, persistent, fire immune, physics has_gravity false has_collision true, box 2.0 x 1.8, int property zoocraft:act 0-15 and bool zoocraft:gliding synced to the client); the bolt zoocraft:void_bolt (a projectile, gravity 0, 5 on a hit, no knock back of its own, 32 x 32 texture). animals/boss.js plays a third kind (KINDS drake; bossRules BOSS_TYPES the literal list): animals/babyDragon.js runs the fight and calm, animals/babyDragonMoves.js steers its flight a tick at a time (mover.js flyStep with its whole box checked; blocked, it climbs over, straight up, level, or slides along one way past a corner; stuck 6 ticks without half the headway it wanted it takes a detour to the nearest spot it can fly straight to, checked every 0.25 blocks, with 4 blocks of room over it), animals/babyDragonRules.js has its numbers (DRAKE). Calm on the nest: asleep 1200-3000 ticks, awake 400-1000, a circle of its lair after 35% of awake spells (320-600 ticks, 8-12 out and 4-8 over the nest); home from under or beside its nest (lower than its top, within 7) out to 7 and up first, then 3 over the nest and down. Provoked by a hit or a Survival/Adventure player within 7 of the ground under it not sneaking: the screech 52 ticks on the nest, the air roar 44 in the air. The fight: circling its target 10-14 out, 5-9 up at 0.3 a tick (the goal lifted 3 over the nest when it or the way to it is within 5.5 of the nest's middle and lower: clearOfNest), a move every 50 ticks (30 angry), none while it is blocked or on a detour; dive 6-26 off (weight 0.45, wind 18 ticks locking at 14, 0.75 a tick, reach 2.5, 7 and knock 1.0/0.4, stunned 40 ticks; only with a clear way for its box to the spot), void bolt 6-28 (0.35, spat at 20 of 30 from its mouth 1.87 ahead and 1.86 up at the chest, speed 1.3, life 80; only with a clear path, checked every 0.1 blocks with 0.06 to spare, an end rod only its rod: lineOpen, pathBlocked, rodBox), wing gust within 20 (0.2, 0.6 with two or more fighting within 6 of its target; 5 out and 3 up in front of them on the first of four sides it can fly straight to with room over it, else a bolt with a clear line; 3 and knock 1.6/0.45 within 8 in an 80 degree arc); angry at 150 (Slowness II 60 ticks within 12 along the ground and 16 up or down, a push), tired at 30 (the glide down to the rest spot at the pillar's foot, given up after 240 ticks), rested after 1200 ticks with nobody within 32 (Date.now() stamp); the void guard (no ground within 24 under the spot 4 along a push: a nudge of 0.2); leash 40 from its nest; put back on its nest if under the world. Lairs (trenchRules drake_lair, babyDragonRules DRAKE_LAIR): on the outer End (more than 800 from 0 0), 800 apart, 32-48 from the player, 160 from parks, end stone ground found going down through chorus (endGroundY), level within 3, 20 of air, no purpur near (drakeSiteLevel); a clearing of radius 8 of end stone with end brick patches, the pillar (3 x 3, 8 high, 14% crying obsidian), the nest at 9 (7 x 7 cut corners, obsidian rim, end bricks, purpur middle, a pinwheel of end stone brick walls, end rods at four corners), three obsidian spikes 6.5 out at the back and back corners (60, 300 and 0 degrees from the back, never in front: the bench found one in its rest spot); the nest spot on the nest (floor + 10), the rest spot 5 in front of the pillar. The zoo one zoocraft:baby_dragon is flown by animals/dragon.js as the Glade's Dragon (its own body, DRAKE_BODY; s.drake), profile great_aerial (900/300, 16 headroom); species endGround minCells 100 (balance endGround comfortCap 55); an empty great_aerial hall is readied by 100 end stone blocks in its floor as by a campfire or gold (exhibits.js countEndFloor); room.js says to lay the end stone; the Clipboard's flight line for an empty hall. The Dragon Crate (zoocraft:dragon_crate, recipe OPO/ECE/OPO obsidian, purpur, ender pearl, Reinforced Crate at the Zoo Workshop; Dragon Tracking research needs 5 ready, 8 purpur blocks, 2 eyes of ender, a compass, 300; bait a Meat Ration); Field Journal tasks drakesleep, drakedive, draketired; Places "Baby Dragon Lairs". The test zoo gets a Baby Dragon hall (31 x 31, end stone floor) and has 59 exhibits. - 0.1.163 (2026-11-01): the Hostile Mob House with zombies and skeletons (module 11, its first part; Austin: "then lets go to the next module"; design research/mobhouse_plan.md; module 10's three bosses are done). The Mob House block zoocraft:mob_house (light 6; recipe BLB/DDD iron bars, lantern, dark oak planks at the Zoo Workshop) in a closed hall (staff/rules.js measureRoom: 3 x 3 x 3 open, up to 3000 cells, measured in a job 50 cells a slice, again every 120 park seconds and after a block change within 3), a guest path to it (network pathLinked), problems outside/inside/open/small/unloaded/path/unchecked (MOB_HOUSE_TEXT); records in equipment (type mobhouse: price 1-30, default 8, carry, paidToday); the menu ui/mobHouseMenu.js. Mob pens: exhibits of mob species (habitatProfile mob_pen: 25 + 10 a mob, 3 headroom) whose floor is within 3 of a working hall (housesNear, BALANCE.mobs.penReach), closed all round (penReport: enclosed, leaks into the hall), mobPenProblem; the pen's floor measured (measureMobFloor; mobFloorShare: zombies 0.5 of dirt/coarse dirt/podzol/rooted dirt/mud, skeletons 0.3 of bone block/cobblestone/mossy cobblestone/cobbled deepslate/gravel; comfort capped at 55 without, welfare ctx mobfloor). Guests: visitors.js visitor step calls checkHouses and mobHouseStep (the step's guests split between working houses; mobHouseSales: share 0.4 at the fair 8, +0.02 a coin under up to 0.6, -0.03 a coin over, times 0.6 + 0.2 a pen on view up to 1; carry the fraction; earn "Mob House: N guests at P" keyed mobhouse:<id>:<step>); pensShown = pens a viewed Viewing Point in the hall looks into. The spawn guard (animals/mobs.js, loop mobguard every 100 ticks rebuilding guardBoxes: working halls and pens grown by 1): a monster spawning in one is removed the next tick. Mob Taming research (needs Zombie and Skeleton observed; 4 rotten flesh, 4 bones, 2 spider eyes, 2 gunpowder, 250). The Passivity Serum zoocraft:passivity_serum (shapeless glass bottle, golden carrot, fermented spider eye -> 2 at the Zoo Workshop; custom component zoocraft:serum: its use aims by getEntitiesFromViewDirection 5, as the game sends no entity interaction for a vanilla zombie, and playerInteractWithEntity takes it too if one comes): serumCheck (MOB_KINDS zombie -> zoocraft:calm_zombie, skeleton -> zoocraft:calm_skeleton; hp at most 0.25 of max, no babies (minecraft:is_baby), other monsters told no); the mob is replaced by its calmed one (same spot, rotation, health, name; slowness 60 ticks; green sparkles, mob.zombie.remedy) and the journal's mobcalm fires. Calmed and zoo mobs: vanilla look (client files name the vanilla geometry, textures, animations and render controllers; the 1.8.0 skeleton client keeps its animation_controllers field, the newer zombie's is dropped), families zoocraft_mob and calm_x / zoo_x (never monster), persistent, no despawn, burns or attacks; calmed: damage only from a player, tempt to their bait; zoo: no damage at all, home groups, a skeleton's bow (equipment table loot_tables/entities/zoocraft_skeleton_bow.json, never dropped); groans and rattles (MOB_SOUNDS, sayChance 0.03 a second). The Mob Cage zoocraft:mob_cage / _occupied (CARRIERS.mob, research mob_taming; recipe BCB/BRB/BBB iron bars, minecraft:iron_chain, Reinforced Crate at the Zoo Workshop: the chain is minecraft:iron_chain since 1.21.110, vanilla-data 1.26.52 has no minecraft:chain); the Bone Ration zoocraft:bone_ration (3 bones + bowl -> 3, crafting table and Zoo Workshop; RATIONS bone, the feeders' food state 14). Species zoocraft:zoo_zombie and zoocraft:zoo_skeleton (mob true, mobSource the vanilla mob, wildSource the calmed one, carrierProfile mob, observe spot + mobcalm, social 1-6, the zombie lowLight; COMMUNITIES crypt); the journal and Clipboard texts; the test zoo's zombie and skeleton blueprints (11 x 11: a pen z 0-6 with the floor, a glass or iron-bars wall, a gravel hall z 8-10 with an oak door, the Mob House block, a Viewing Point at 5 8; 61 exhibits). Bench (BDS 1.26.60, Easy, seed 12345, flat, noon; bdstools/mob_bp, mob163a.sh to mob163e.sh, dbg163.py, steps_mob1 to steps_mob5): runs A to E as in docs/Mob_House_test_plan.md; found there: the Mob Cage recipe's old chain id (fixed); the houses read outside the park until the first visitor step after a test-zoo build (the block is placed before the fence closes; self-heals, a player's placing is checked at once). 329 tests (one new). The grab: animals/voidling.js (startGrab, tickGrabs every tick in main.js's voidgrab loop: both teleported along the way, the player's look kept; out by edge, broken (grabHit from boss.js's before-hurt on a player's hit), escaped (leash), stuck, timeout, lost; endGrab when the fight ends, it tires, is calmed or is gone). Fixes found on the bench: boss.js's thrown-hit handler read the projectile's velocity after it was gone (InvalidEntityError, so no Darkness and no guarded push since 0.1.162): it reads the event's hitVector now, and pushes only the guarded ones (the Baby Dragon's bolt, the Voidling's orb; the others push by the game's own impact knockback, as benched); Block.isSolid is not in @minecraft/server 2.10 (it read undefined): the way to the edge (columnReader), the drag's room check (roomAt) and the lairs' fill under their floors (since 0.1.159) use data/blocks.js PASSABLE_PATTERNS now, and a test fails on any script reading isSolid; with the columns read for real, a look that found no rift site on a big island took 0.47 seconds in one tick, so the rift look is a job on the lairs' queue now (riftLook, makeRift a generator: a slice a site and a way round it, riftNearEdgeSteps; one a player at a time; the lairs read afresh before a rift is made). getTopmostBlock reads under water (the bench: the end stone under a Chorus Meadow's pond, gravel under the sea), so a rift cut beside a pond was flooded: wetReader reads the block over a column's top, and voidRules.js dryRound keeps a rift's floor and 3 round it (RIFT.dry) and the Baby Dragon's clearing and 3 round it (makeDrakeLair) clear of water. - 0.1.169 (2026-11-01): wolves and foxes (module 12, the vanilla catalogue, its second part; Austin: "then lets go to the next module"; design research/vanilla_plan.md "0.1.169 wolves and foxes: the design"). zoocraft:zoo_wolf and zoo_fox: zoo copies of the game's own (the farmyard four's pattern: origin vanilla, wildSource, the vanilla client entities renamed, spawn egg dropped; the zoo's own server files with the vanilla health, speed, size and baby; no tameable, angry, attack targets, avoid_mob_type, breedable, despawn or pickup), the coat the wild one's minecraft:variant (wolves 9, foxes 2). A zoo wolf keeps the game's beg and tempt (Meat Rations and the raw meats) and visits the zoocraft:scent_post blocks in a cycle: component group zoocraft:scent_rest (a minecraft:timer of 30-90 s) then zoocraft:scent_seek (behavior.move_to_block, stay_duration 100, its on_stay_completed or a 15-25 s timer firing zoocraft:scent_done back to the rest; move_to_block alone kept them at the posts 66-100% of the time on the bench); look_at_player and random_stroll keep the game's priorities (6, 8; random_look_around 10). A zoo fox keeps the game's day/night/thunderstorm groups with behavior.nap (can_nap: on the ground, out of water, is_underground; woken by anyone but a trusted one, a fox, a sneaking player, or the zoo's staff, guests and animals: families zoocraft_staff, zoocraft_guest, zoocraft_animal) and find_cover, and its ambient groups; random_stroll, look_at_player and random_look_around keep the game's priorities (13, 14, 15: the zoo's usual 6-8 came before the nap, and on the bench zoo foxes hardly slept). Sounds: a new RP sounds.json (entity_sounds by the zoo copy's identifier) for the wolf, fox, rabbit, sheep, cow, pig and chicken from the game's own, babies keyed on query.is_baby (no minecraft:sound_variant variants: a pack can't declare it), and ambient_sound_interval on the copies that had none. The Field Journal's care and catching lines (woodlandCareLines, woodlandCatchLine). The test zoo's Wolves run and Foxes den (bench/parkPlan.js; 72 exhibits). - 0.1.170 (2026-11-01): pandas (module 12, the vanilla catalogue, its third part; Austin: "then lets go to the next module", "Plan's picks" on the order card: polar bears moved to 0.1.171) and fixes for Austin's content log (2026-11-01 15:00Z). zoocraft:zoo_panda: a zoo copy of the game's own (the 0.1.168 pattern: origin vanilla, wildSource minecraft:panda, the vanilla client entity renamed, spawn egg dropped; the zoo's own server file with the vanilla health, speed, size and baby; no breedable, despawn, angry, attack or roll), the coat the gene that shows (minecraft:variant 0-6), each gene a component group added by its own event (zoocraft:coat_N: lazy lay_down, random_sitting, snacking, movement 0.07; worried avoid_mob_type of players, 16 and 20; weak health 10; the rest the variant only). Gene events only add: an event that removes component groups an entity never had corrupted the components (on the bench every panda took the lazy speed and the weak health). behavior.roll left out: on a custom entity it flung the panda up and away (y climbing about 0.06 a tick, 2.2 blocks a second sideways) on the bench; behavior.sneeze kept on cubs (slime balls, small safe hops). A released panda gets the game's own gene event (animals/bambooRules.js GENE_EVENTS; transport/carriers.js). - 0.1.173 (2026-11-01): the Mythical Glade's leaves flashed as Austin moved (his message of 20:05Z, screenshot at 318, 100, 720; the same day he confirmed 0.1.172 cleared the Locator lines). Cause: the four leaf blocks (the glade's mythwood and moonwillow leaves, the End's whitewood and white_willow leaves; full_block geometry) used render_method alpha_test_to_opaque, which turns backface culling off. Two leaf blocks side by side each keep their shared face (a see-through neighbour culls nothing), so two faces lie in one plane, both drawn, with mirrored UVs: z-fighting, flashing stripes as the camera moves (Mojang on MCPE-137976: coplanar textures z-fight with any render method). And past half the render distance the method draws the block opaque, showing the holes' stored RGB, which was (0,0,0): black specks far off. Fix: render_method alpha_test_single_sided_to_opaque on all four (the same near/far switch with backface culling on: only the face turned to the camera draws, so one face of each shared pair); the holes keep alpha 0 and take one darker shade of their leaf (the mean of its darkest quarter, as the game's leaves_oak_opaque fills its holes with the oak leaf's darkest shade 104,99,104): mythwood 207,116,177; whitewood 206,197,219; moonwillow 57,165,154; white willow 194,195,206. Rejected: culling rules (need a custom geometry and would hollow the canopies), culling_layer leaves (experimental). Inferred from the docs and the screenshot, the cloud can't draw blocks: Austin's restart confirms. 340 tests (one new: no block uses alpha_test_to_opaque; the four leaves' holes one non-black shade darker than the leaf; it fails on 0.1.172). How: gen173/ (fix173.py, tests173.py, test173.mjs, docs173.py, ship173.py). A full Minecraft restart. Test plan: docs/Leaves_test_plan.md. Next: 0.1.174 polar bears (young drawn the same way).

The plan's 10 park days run with the game's own pure formulas (gen178/model178.mjs: demand, rating, fair price, arrivals, snack, donation, gift, tour, ride and Mob House sales, wages, and each exhibit's food at shop prices from the test zoo's blueprints and the species' ration units) for the starter park's rabbits, 3, 6 and 12 exhibits and the whole test zoo (68 exhibits with animals). Found: guests stopped growing at 3 exhibits (dailyDemand's attraction capped at 1.0), so at 4.5 stars a 12-exhibit park drew 18 guests a day like a 3-exhibit one while its food cost 343 coins a day against 44, and lost coins whenever it bought its food; the test zoo drew 22 guests and its food cost 1,219 a day. Also: crafted rations sold back to the supply shop without limit (1 coin each; a hay block makes 9 Grain Rations, 4 bamboo or 4 kelp make 2 Leafy Rations), so a crop farm could out-earn the zoo (inferred from the recipes; no farm was benched). Fixed: dailyDemand adds speciesDraw (visitors/demand.js): each kind on show in open exhibits past the 3rd adds a tenth of demandTarget, up to 30 kinds (balance.js economy drawPerSpecies 0.1, drawFromSpecies 3, drawSpeciesCap 30; visitors.js ratingNow counts shownSpecies and passes it to both demand reads); with it 6 exhibits draw 24 guests (+247 a day buying all food, was +117), 12 draw 34 (+294, was a loss), the test zoo 80 (+713 buying all food, +1,932 crafting it). Parks of 1 to 3 kinds are unchanged. The supply shop buys back at most 64 rations a park day (economy sellBackPerDay; ledger.js sellBackLeft; park.today.soldBack, reset by rollDay; the Sell rations page and slider say what's left today and check again after the slider). Checked and left: the zero-funds path (spec AC-18) stays playable, as admissions don't need coins, unpaid keepers stop work and say so (staff.js), rations and carriers can be crafted, and the ledger refuses to go below zero; the fair ticket price is close to the best-paying one (each coin over it costs 3% of guests and 8 value points); conservation rewards are bounded (one release a kind per cooldown); tours lose a little under about 25 guests a day (a guide's 30 coins against 6 a ticket) and pay above it. 345 tests (one new). The pack checker's 156 warnings sorted against Mojang's bedrock-samples v1.26.60.5 (commit 15701fa9): 128 of the 129 vanilla animation names are Mojang's own, the 14 texture sizes are drawings at twice the geometry's size on purpose, the 12 snapping states are routing states or the game's own, the bare-number head scale is the game's own sheep's; the one name not in Mojang's files, animation.sheep.setup.v2, was mapped by zoo_sheep.entity.json and never played: dropped (155 warnings, none a bug). Budgets (Mojang's "Improving performance" guidance: 4,000 texture handles in all, under 800 for an add-on, under 2,000 for a world template; add-ons under 25 MB and 2,500 files): about 176 entity texture handles, 17.1 MB, 2,172 files (about 330 left for the vanilla animals still to come); no unused textures, animations, controllers, render controllers or particles; six cub geometries in zoo_cubs.geo.json unused since 0.1.172 and two retired scripts (world/trench.js, animals/push.js) never loaded, kept (files are never deleted on the laptop). The lang file: every entity, spawn egg, item and block named, no duplicates or orphans. The whole test zoo on the test server (BDS 1.26.60, 76 exhibits, 225 animals, 5 minutes): no content-log errors, 22 guests, all animals in their exhibits; 15 exhibits warned on the Clipboard, each read block by block with a bench probe: (1) 8 exhibits' "N blocks of floor are under a roof or blocks lower than H blocks ... Raise the roof" counted the tops of things standing on the floor (troughs, a feeder's basket, Thermal Regulators, a low perch, the Chorus Moths' lamp, the soul campfire, sculk blocks) and the floor under the Void Rays' hanging Void Perches, under roofs already at the headroom. exhibits/geometry.js topsOf: a low floor cell a block up from a measured cell that has the headroom (the floor; in a flight room also the air or a perch) is a top, counted as out.lowTopCells, and in a flight room the floor under a hanging perch too (it still costs its column, 0.1.67's rule); ex.lowCells and ex.aerial.lowCells take off the larger of lowTopCells and the Chorus Moths' and dragons' own counts (never both). lowCeilingCells and usable areas are unchanged. (2) 7 exhibits had no enrichment: the test zoo's Soul Sloths, Earth Molebears, Skulkers, cows, pigs, sheep and chickens get a Hanging Food Basket over the feeder (Grain Rations for the farmyard, so the Staff Rooms refill it). (3) The hedgehogs' lamps hung 3 up inside their 3-block headroom: they stand on the floor by the walls (bench/parkPlan.js floorLamp, block_face up); the Soul Moths' lantern hangs over their campfire; the Chorus Moths' lamp was set with the default block_face (down: upside down on the floor) and now stands up. BENCH_VERSION 3 (the benchmark's soul moth and chorus moth exhibits changed; the same 922 operations). (4) The tigers' "2 grown tigers are loose together and fighting" was the bench player 130 blocks away (tiger.js KEEP_RANGE 56: they walk to a den only near a player); with the player beside them one was in her Holding Den within 60 seconds. The fixed test zoo re-run on the test server: no content-log errors, all 76 exhibits occupied with their 225 animals, 19 guests in the 5 minutes; the Clipboard warns only for the tigers (the bench player far off, as above) and 3 floor blocks in the Chorus Moths' house that their chorus grew over during those 5 minutes (a true warning: trim it); the hedgehogs' house has all 80 floor blocks lit, brightest 6, no daylight, and the Soul Moths' all 36 lit, brightest 9 of their 10. 346 tests. Scripts and one edited client file: leave the world and join again. The test plan is docs/Asset_pass_test_plan.md. Module 13 is done; next, module 12's vanilla animals from the goats.
- 0.1.180 (2026-11-05): goats (module 12, the first of its vanilla animals after module 13). `zoocraft:zoo_goat`, the game's own as a zoo copy (health 10, 0.9 by 1.3, speed 0.4, the game's sounds; its goat.geo.json and skin drawn the 0.1.172 way: one skeleton, the kid at the server's 0.5 with no client scale, both horns always on through the zoo's own render controller, since the game's horns hang on engine variables only its own goat gets). A screamer (the game's variant 1, coat 1 here) screams where others bleat (ambient.screamer by the game's own query.variant condition on the ambient sound; hurt.screamer and death.screamer are mapped in sounds.json too, but whether the engine picks them for an entity that isn't minecraft:goat is not confirmed) and milking one plays milk.screamer (the game's own interact); a kid takes the coat after one parent or the other; no ram_attack, jump_to_block, breedable, genetics or rideable. The test server first: the game's own goats, 6 to a pen, leapt out of walls 2, 3, 4, 5, 6 and 8 high within 2 minutes (behavior.jump_to_block searches 10 up), so the zoo goat's leaps are the script's (animals/goat.js, the End Hopper's arc: up to 3 up, 4 across, 6 down; loops "goats" every 10 ticks at offset 147 and "goatmove" every tick), only onto ledges measured inside an exhibit that's enclosed for goats. The mountain exhibit (profile mountain 60/16, 3 high; rules in animals/goatRules.js, BALANCE.goat): the goats' own walk (exhibits/geometry.js floodTerrestrial with opts.rise 3 and opts.leapFences: a fence or closed gate is a block to stand on) must not get out, so walls need 4 blocks over anything a goat can stand on beside them (wallsIssue: "Not enclosed for goats", where they could get out); rock ledges (ledgeReport: the top of a rock block, data/blocks.js ROCK_PATTERNS, with a drop of 2 beside it; 2 a goat, 4 at least, one a lookout 4 up; without them comfort caps at 60; ex.goat = enclosed, escape, ledges, high, top, the 64 highest spots); shelter (the farmyard's hay under a roof, 1 for 2); a Hay Rack; a keeper on its round makes it a petting farm. Their day: the game walks them; a grown one leaps onto a ledge it can reach (the highest 60% of the time), stands looking out toward the drop (act 2), then leaps on or back down; two grown ones 1 to 2.5 apart on the ground now and then butt heads (act 3: the game's ram animation, the clash sound, both pushed back). The client reads zoocraft:act (0 life, 1 leap, 2 perch, 3 butt). Field Journal: spot, herd, and a new task, watch one leap onto a ledge (a wild one in the air off its slopes). Caught with wheat or a Grain Ration in a Livestock Crate; let go, the game's own born_default or born_screamer event keeps its coat and the release grows it up. Test server (BDS 1.26.60): the test zoo's Goats measured ready (155 blocks, 12 rock ledges, a lookout 4 up, 2 hay bales under the roof, no problems); 3 minutes of their day: 148 leaps (113 onto ledges, 35 back down), every landing on a ledge or the floor inside (feet at most 4 up, the arcs peaking 5.0 over the floor), none carried, none out; at the usual chances 10 leaps in a minute for the four; head-butts with leaps off at a raised chance: 11 in 60 s, each a bow of 36 ticks, the clash, both pushed back; the top block of the walls taken off (3 high): "Not enclosed for goats ... They could get out near 24 -60 -2" (state unsafe; the clipboard's Mountain line had counted 112 "ledges" on the wall tops and beyond, so it now says "not enclosed for goats, so no leaps"), no leaps in 30 s at the raised chance; the walls back: ready again; a game screamer (born_screamer) caught with wheat in a Livestock Crate by the carrier's use (the simulated player's interactWithEntity returns false on a goat: no event, as 0.1.175's horses) and let out in the Goats: coat 1, variant 1; 20 zoo goats of each coat let go by the release's own lines: the right coat and grown on all 40 from 1 tick on (2 young at once before the events, the game's 5 in 100); a game goat spawned 60 blocks from the only player was gone within 5 ticks (its minecraft:despawn, inferred), so the bench stood the player beside it; no content-log errors. 347 tests (one new; older ones widened for 77 species, the comfort context's ledges, the exhibit measure's goat walk, the enclosed line, the Hay Rack's eaters, the bedding warning's shelter, the catch line, the clipboard's farm line, the sound keys and the young's scales). How: gen180/ (ents180.py, wire180.py, goatRules.js, goat.js, tests180.py, test180.mjs, docs180.py, ship180.py). A full Minecraft restart. Test plan: docs/Goats_test_plan.md. Next: 0.1.181 camels (shade, sand), the next habitat in research/vanilla_plan.md's order.
- 0.1.181 (2026-11-05): camels (module 12). `zoocraft:zoo_camel`, the game's own as a zoo copy (health 32, 1.7 by 2.375, the game's sounds and its camel.geo.json and skin drawn the 0.1.172 way: one skeleton, the calf at the server's 0.45 with no client scale; the zoo's own render controller with no saddle, bridle or reins; the zoo's own copies of the game's two animation controllers from before 26.10, with the dash left out, run from the client entity's scripts.animate, since the old animation_controllers field is format 1.8.0 only). No saddle, riding, dash or breeding by the game. The test server first (probes 1 and 2): the game's own sitting goal (behavior.random_look_around_and_sit) never sat a zoo copy in about 40 camel-minutes, with or without is_tamed, while the game's camels sat 8 times in 9; behavior.find_cover never moved a zoo copy (moved 0.0) while the game's foxes found cover and slept; and the game's camels step over oak fences (variable_max_auto_step 1.5625), so the copy has none. Inferred: those goals run only for the game's own mob. So the camel's day is the script's (animals/camel.js, rules in animals/camelRules.js, BALANCE.camel; loops "camels" every 20 ticks at offset 149 and "camelmove" every tick): now and then a kneel a while (sit, act 1, at about the game's rate); at midday (4000 to 9000) a grown one out in the sun is walked into the shade (zoocraft:lead takes its own walk away; a push a tick along a way over the exhibit's measured floor, zebraRules' findWay and smoothWay, to the nearest spot shaded all round, from the exhibit measure's own list of shaded floor, exhibits/geometry.js shadeSpots and exhibits.js shadeSpotsOf, kept in memory beside its cells; not beside another camel's), goes on to that spot past the shade's edge, and kneels there (rest) till the heat passes, giving up for a minute if stuck or after 30 s; out of the heat, on sand, now and then a dust bath (act 2: sand thrown up round it, the sand's sound). The desert exhibit (profile desert 120/40, 3 high): sand or red sand under half the usable floor (exhibits/geometry.js sandFloorCells) and shade over 6 floor blocks a camel (the covered cells the measure already counts, 3 or more up), each capping comfort at 60 without; a Hay Rack, a Basic Feeder or an Elevated Browse Feeder of Leafy Rations; a cactus is a Leafy Ration's worth. Every one of the game's camels is tamed from birth, so species.bornTamed lets the catch past the tamed check. Field Journal: spot, herd, and a new task, watch one kneel (a zoo one at rest or in a dust bath; a wild one by the game's own minecraft:start_sitting, which the script sees). Caught with a cactus or a Leafy Ration in a Livestock Crate; let go, it comes out grown by the game's own minecraft:spawn_adult. The test zoo gets a Camels exhibit. Test server (BDS 1.26.60): the test zoo's Camels measured ready (223 usable blocks, 190 on sand, 63 shaded by the roof, a Hay Rack, no problems); their own life in the morning (each walked 16 to 44 blocks in a minute by the game's own stroll, now and then knelt 6 to 31 s, one dust bath on sand), none out; at midday each grown one in the sun was walked into the shade (a push a tick with its own walk taken away moved it at 0.1 a tick: 9 blocks in 6 s) and knelt there, 51 to 59 s of each minute, at two noons; first it knelt as soon as it stepped under the roof's edge (half its body in the sun), so a walking camel now goes on to its spot (shaded all round, the measure's own list of shaded floor), and all three then lay front, middle and back under the roof; the heat past (9500), all stood up and walked again within a second; at raised chances (bench only) 8 kneels and 3 dust baths in a minute; 128 floor blocks turned to grass (63 of 112 sand): the clipboard's Desert line "too little" and the sand warning on the welfare pass after; the roof taken off (0 of 18 shaded): the shade warning, and at midday no walks; both put back: ready again and into the shade; a game camel (tamed from birth, as all of them) caught with a cactus in a Livestock Crate by the carrier's use and let out in the Camels; 4 game camels watched 2 minutes: 7 kneels by their own sitting, each seen by the script (minecraft:start_sitting and stop_sitting); 20 zoo camels let go by the release's own lines: all grown at once and 1, 3 and 10 ticks on (spawn_adult); let go 65 blocks from the only player they were gone within a tick (the game camel's despawn_from_distance), so the bench stood the player by them; no content-log errors. 348 tests (one new; older ones widened for 78 species, the comfort context's sand and shade, the exhibit measure's sand, the cover height, the Hay Rack's eaters, the catch line, the release's grown list and the sound keys). How: gen181/ (ents181.py, wire181.py, camelRules.js, camel.js, tests181.py, test181.mjs, docs181.py, ship181.py). A full Minecraft restart. Test plan: docs/Camels_test_plan.md. Next: 0.1.182 turtles (water, with the tanks), the next habitat in research/vanilla_plan.md's order.
- 0.1.182 (2026-11-05): NPC residents, the first part (module 6c, idea 15; Austin's "next module", the card's default ahead of the rest of the vanilla animals). Named people who live in houses the players build and walk to the zoo every day. Four stand-ins (data/residents.js: Rosie Fernwood, Captain Brindle, Pip Hollow, Dr. Amara Vale, each with a look, a bio, likes, dislikes and lines) until Austin's 32 come. A home (residents/homes.js, rules in residents/homeRules.js, BALANCE.residents): a Resident Mailbox (new block: a blue box on a post with a red flag, a chest over an oak fence at the Zoo Workshop; its placer owns the home) inside a closed house or on its outside wall, the Staff Room's room check (staff/rules.js measureRoom, now able to hand back its cells: 3 x 3 x 3 open inside, not counting the bed), a bed of its own and a door in its walls (the one nearest the mailbox), and a path from the door: inside a park's fence the door's path must be on that park's guest paths; outside every fence path blocks from the door must reach the path by a Park Entrance Gate (up to 4000 path blocks, walked as a job, saved under "paths" home:<id> and remembered where unloaded). Each home is checked again every 60 s and at once when a block changes in it, by it or on its path; a broken mailbox (or one gone without a player) takes the home and its resident with it. The mailbox's menu says whether the home works and what's missing (a checklist), who lives there and what they're doing, offers three residents to an empty working home, and lets its owner ask them to move out. The resident (new entity zoocraft:resident: the guests' model, four looks by the zoocraft:who property, the name always shown, a sleep pose lying back from the waist; no pushable, despawn or navigation, fire-immune, unhurt) is walked by the script (residents/residents.js; loops "homes" and "residents" every 20 ticks, "residentwalk" every tick: a step of 0.11 a tick, the guests' pace) with a body only within 48 blocks of a player and on without one further away. Their day by the time of day (residents/residentRules.js): each sets off at their own time between 1000 and 3000, opens the front door, steps out, shuts it, walks the path, pays the zoo's admission at the gate (once a world day: the takings and the day's guests), visits Viewing Points, benches and stands on the guest paths (15 to 35 s at each), heads home at 11500 the same way, in at the door, and lies down in the bed at 13000; up at 23000 (or when a skipped night brings the morning). The zoo shut: they stay home. The house broken (bed or door gone, a wall open): home, and they wait outside the door until it works again. Talking to one opens their page (a line, bio, home, what they're doing, likes and dislikes). Test server (BDS 1.26.60, three rounds, the test zoo and a 4 x 3 x 3 stone house 8 blocks south of its gate with a gravel path, a bed and an oak door): the home checked ready (33 blocks of space, the bed, the door, its way 2 points to the gate); a second invite and a second resident for the same home refused; moving in, she appeared at the front door, opened it, walked in, shut it and stood inside (first the door stayed shut, acted on before her body had spawned: the door now acts whenever a player is in range, body or not); the mailbox menu read ready with the checklist and who lives there; at 3500 out the door (opened from inside, shut behind her), along the path, paid 10 at the gate, then round the Viewing Points and the stand; the player sent 200 blocks away: the body went at once and she walked on without it (23 blocks in 10 s, as with one), then had a body again where she'd got to, 1 block from her door, and went in; at 11500 home the same way; at 13000 to the bed, lying on it facing the foot; /time set 0: up and to her spot; next day out again and paid again; the bed taken out while she was at the zoo: the home said no bed within a tick, she walked home and waited outside the shut door, the mailbox saying why; the bed back: in within a second; the mailbox removed without a player: the home and resident gone within 6 s; a house inside the fence with a gravel spur to the zoo's path: Captain Brindle stepped out, shut the door and paid on the step, walked the zoo's paths and came back to the door at 11500 (first its check said "no path" for a minute, run before the park's own path walk had taken the spur in: a home in a park now waits for that walk, visitors/network.js pathsPending, and checks again when it ends, "pathsChanged": round 3 read "checking" then ready 37 ticks later); each body's look held (who 1 and 3 for Captain Brindle and Dr. Amara Vale, a tick after spawning; setProperty reads back the default in the spawn tick); the bench player's use on a resident fires no playerInteractWithEntity (before or after), as 0.1.175's horses, so her page was opened from the script (the use itself is the guests' handler in main.js, inferred to work for a real player); no content-log errors. 349 tests (one new: the home's parts, the bed spot, the problems in order, the day table, the lines, the entity, block, recipe and wiring). How: gen182/ (wire182.py, ents182.py, fix182a.py, fix182b.py, fix182c.py, docs182.py, ship182.py; the bench bdstools/dbg182.py, rs182.sh, steps_rs182, b and c). A new block and entity: a full Minecraft restart. Test plan: docs/Residents_test_plan.md. Next: 0.1.183 likes, dislikes and mood (research/npc_plan.md), then Austin's residents as his list comes.
- 0.1.183 (2026-11-05): sea turtles (module 12, Austin's "Vanilla animals" on 2026-11-05: the residents' likes, dislikes and mood move to after the vanilla animals). `zoocraft:zoo_turtle`, the game's own as a zoo copy (health 30, 1.2 by 0.4, its amphibious moves, swim and crawl, sounds, model and skin; the young drawn the 0.1.172 way: the grown model at the server's 0.3, no client scale; the zoo's own render controller and the game's two animation controllers copied, the egg belly by act 1 and the digging flippers by act 2). No breeding, egg laying, going home or despawning by the game: the zoo's nursery and the script do the nesting. The test server first (bdstools/tv183): a copy restricted to its home (minecraft:home random_movement, radius 8) never once went ashore or rose to the surface in 5 minutes, while one with the game's own plain home (no limit) went ashore 9 to 17 times in 2.5 minutes, as did one with radius 32; the aquatic family made no difference; So the zoo's homes on the turtle limit nothing (the exhibit's walls keep it in); inferred: the camels' "goals do nothing for a copy" (0.1.181) may have been the same home limit. Then no turtle at all (the zoo's, the game's own, a copy the zoo has no record of) ever came ashore from the test zoo's Turtle Beach, nor from a plain pen 2 deep at another spot, while five copies of the beach elsewhere (with or without seagrass, fixtures, walls of stone 3 high or glass 2 high) had 3 to 7 ashore each in 150 s and natural pens 1 to 3 deep 2 to 13 (bdstools/tq183: not the shape, depth, seagrass, fixtures or walls; the cause unexplained), so the game's move_to_land is unreliable by place and the script hauls them out to bask: by day a grown turtle in the water has a 0.01 chance a second (BALANCE.turtle baskChance; a minute's rest after each) to be walked up onto the sand within 10 blocks of her and 2 of the water (pickBaskSpot), lie there 20 to 50 s (still, zoocraft:dig's act) and be walked back (nextBaskPhase). A sea turtle's hatchlings, like the manatees' calves, need no sheltered corner (the nursery's inWater; the bench found breeding stopped for want of one). The turtle beach (profile turtle_beach 45/45, 2 high, at least 60% water, 20% dry, 27 deep): seagrass or kelp in 4 water columns each (the manatees' rule, comfort capped at 55 without), sand 2 to 5 from the water to nest in (the crocodiles' measure, ex.sandSites), greens from an Aquarium Feeder. Breeding: 1 to 4 eggs after 3 park days, grown in 8, rest 6; the nest record made as they mate (marked turtle, the clutch); at night (13000 to 23000), and by day on the day the eggs are due (so a world that sleeps the nights away still sees her lay; Kemp's ridleys nest by day), the script walks her (zoocraft:lead, a push a tick along a way over the exhibit's walked cells, animals/turtle.js, rules in animals/turtleRules.js, BALANCE.turtle; loops "turtles" every 20 ticks at offset 151 and "turtlemove" every tick) up the beach to the nest spot, she digs (zoocraft:dig, act 2, sand thrown up, dig.sand) and lays (the nursery's layTurtleEggs: the Turtle Nest block, zoocraft:turtle_nest, eggs 1 to 4 and stage 0 to 2 by bone visibility, the game's egg textures, walked through, drops nothing), then is walked back to the nearest water. In the water a push a tick hardly moved a led turtle (the bench: one walked to the beach stuck at its edge), so there she is carried a step a tick along the way (turtleCarry by tryTeleport, the walk's pace across and up to swimRise 0.08 up or down), and on land pushed as before. She's 1.2 wide, so beside a wall or at the beach's edge a teleport to a block's middle is refused (the bench: every walk stuck there): she slides along x or z alone instead, climbs out of the water at the edge up to climbRise 0.3 a tick (the bench: she rose out at the edge and went over), and goes on past a point of the way's cut corners she can't reach (carryStalled: the bench found the last water point 0.47 short of her at the edge); then 4 walks of 4 reached the sand. A walk that sticks tries again later, and on the due night she lays where she has got to. The nest cracks two days before the hatch and more on the last (the egg crack sound), hatches with the shells' break and the baby's born sound, and is gone when the young are out; a nest in full sun hatches 80% females, a shaded one 80% males; nobody guards it. A young one grown sheds a Turtle Scute (now, or when it next loads). Field Journal: spot, and two new tasks, watch one come ashore (on sand out of the water) and watch one dig her nest (a zoo one at act 2, a wild one between the game's own minecraft:go_lay_egg and laid_egg). Caught with seagrass or a Leafy Ration in a Livestock Crate; let go, it comes out grown (the game's grow-up event). The test zoo gets a Turtle Beach (14 by 12, a pool 2 deep with 16 columns of seagrass, a sand beach, walls 3 high: the unit test of its measure found a Water Filter against a wall 2 high was a step up and over it). Test server (BDS 1.26.60): the full cycle (bdstools/tt183.sh, run tt183e): the Turtle Beach measured ready (108 water, all 2 deep, 16 with seagrass, 48 sand spots to nest in, the Aquarium Feeder, the clipboard's "Nesting beach: yes"); 4 bask walks of 4 reached the sand, lay there and came back, slipping into the water at the edge; a game turtle caught with seagrass in a Livestock Crate and let out in the Turtle Beach; the pair mated (no sheltered corner needed), she carried her eggs (act 1), and at night her first walk stuck by the Water Filter on the beach's corner, the next reached her spot: she dug (act 2) and laid an egg, the Turtle Nest showing it, cracking twice as the days passed, then hatching (in full sun: a female) and going, the hatchling in the water at once; grown, it shed a Turtle Scute (the item on the floor); 20 zoo turtles let go came out grown a tick on; no content-log errors. Earlier runs found breeding stopped by the sheltered-corner rule, every walk out of the water stuck at the edge, and the catch refused for room once four turtles lived there (the bench now catches first). 350 tests (one new; older ones widened for 79 species, the young's scale, the nest's clutch, the hatch sexes, the seagrass and Aquarium Feeder rules and text, the catch line, the release's grown list and the sound keys). How: gen183/ (ents183.py, wire183.py, turtleRules.js, turtle.js, tests183.py, test183.mjs, docs183.py, ship183.py; the benches bdstools/tp183.sh, tv183/, tq183/, dbg183.py, tt183.sh, steps_tt183.txt, and tt183w.sh with dbgcarry.py for the carry). A new entity and a new block: a full Minecraft restart. Test plan: docs/Sea_Turtles_test_plan.md. Next: 0.1.184 frogs, the next habitat in research/vanilla_plan.md's order (axolotls, dolphins, bees, parrots, ocelots, armadillos, mooshrooms, sniffers, shulkers after), then the residents' likes, dislikes and mood (research/npc_plan.md).
- 0.1.184 (2026-11-06): frogs and tadpoles (module 12, Austin's "Just keep working until all the vanilla mobs are finished": the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_frog`, the game's frog as a zoo copy (its model, three skins by climate: temperate orange 0, cold green 1, warm white 2; its hops, swimming, croaking and leaps onto lily pads and big dripleaf, its sounds), and `zoocraft:zoo_tadpole`, the game's tadpole as a mob of its own (one model each, the 0.1.172 lesson). Rules in animals/frogRules.js, BALANCE.frog. The frog pond (profile frog_pond: 36 blocks for the first frog and 18 more each, a quarter water, half dry land, 3 of headroom; from the EAZA natterjack toad guideline's breeding enclosure, its water 20 cm deep, so 1 deep is enough): a lily pad or big dripleaf on the water for every 2 frogs (comfort capped at 60 without), cover on land, Insect Rations from a feeder. Their leaps: the test server's frogs got over walls 2, 3 and 4 high from the floor and never over 5 or 6 in 8 minutes, so the exhibit measure follows a frog's leap (geometry.js floodTerrestrial's new leap option: onto the top of anything up to 4 up and 8 across, fences and gates too, unless a column between is solid across the leap; the 8 across is the cautious reading of the game's jump_to_block search_width, inferred: a sideways bench was too rare to measure) and walls need 5 of height over anything a frog can sit on within 8 blocks; leap reads under the world's floor are skipped (the game read the pond as not enclosed with no place named until fixed). Breeding: rain gets a pair mating at once (one good day on a rainy day); she lays frogspawn on the open water, beside a lily pad first (the Frog Spawn block, zoocraft:frog_spawn, the game's look, walked through, drops nothing, comes back if broken while hers), and breeding waits for open water so the tadpoles never come out on land; it hatches in 2 park days into 2 to 6 tadpoles that swim and graze the pond (fed while it has its lily pads, no feeder), and in 4 park days each grows into the frog of the pond's climate (the game's rule from the biome's tags) with the game's grow-up sound. The test server found the game's own tadpoles and a copy lying still in water 1 deep (the game's random_swim skips surface water: avoid_surface) and swimming in water 2 deep; the zoo tadpole's own swim goal (avoid_surface off, targets within 5 across and 2 up or down, a 1 in 40 chance a tick) swam in both. A frog pond's Hanging Food Basket hangs up to 4 blocks over the floor (they leap up at it; under a tree's canopy, where a frog can't sit on it): the bench found a basket there unserved under the 2-block rule, both in serves() and in the exhibit's box for fixtures. Caught with an Insect Ration (slimeballs tempt them, but aren't the bait) in a Small Carrier; let go, a frog gets its look back by the game's spawn events (spawn_temperate 0, spawn_cold 1, spawn_warm 2, confirmed on the test server). Field Journal: spot one, watch one sit on a lily pad, watch one leap. The test zoo gets a Frog Pond (10 by 8, a pool 1 deep with two lily pads, walls 6 high, an oak canopy 6 up on a trunk with the basket under it, a Water Filter, a Basic Feeder of Insect Rations). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, run c4): the pond measured ready with no warnings; the pair mated in the rain on the first day, laid frogspawn on the water, it hatched into 2 tadpoles that swam all over the pond, and both grew into orange frogs that leapt about; no content-log errors. 352 tests. How: the bench tools in the shared folder's bench/ (b184: steps_leap, steps_reach2, steps_c, steps_tad, steps_tad2, steps_tad3 and probe_ent). New entities and a new block: a full Minecraft restart. Test plan: docs/Frogs_test_plan.md. Next: 0.1.185 axolotls, then the rest of research/vanilla_plan.md's order (hostile mobs from 0.1.198).
- 0.1.185 (2026-11-06): axolotls (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_axolotl`, the game's axolotl as a zoo copy (its model, five skins by minecraft:variant: 0 pink "lucy", 1 cyan, 2 gold, 3 brown "wild", 4 blue; its amphibious moves, swimming and its walk along the bottom, its sounds; the young drawn the 0.1.172 way, the grown model at the server's 0.5). Rules in animals/axolotlRules.js, BALANCE.axolotl. The axolotl tank (profile axolotl_tank: 16 blocks of water for the first and 4 more each, most of the exhibit water, 1 deep is enough; from the EAZA Amphibian TAG's best practice guideline for the Lake Patzcuaro salamander, a close relative kept the same way: a densely planted tank, a bare or fine sand floor because gravel is swallowed, dim light and plant cover, eggs laid one by one on water plants). Water plants: seagrass or kelp growing in 2 of the tank's water blocks for each (a young one half), counted as the manatees' seagrass is; they're the axolotls' cover, their enrichment (enrichmentTags empty, the manatees' way: the test server's first run asked for a Floating Toy though the plants were there) and where she lays her eggs; without enough comfort is capped at 60 and there's no breeding. The floor: the exhibit measure goes down each column of water to its floor (exhibits.js, axolotlRules.gravelFloors) and gravel there caps comfort at 60 with a warning naming a spot. Food: Insect Rations from an Aquarium Feeder (which now takes Insect Rations too). Breeding: one young (a larva) hatches 2 park days after mating and grows up in 5; it takes a parent's colour, or 1 time in 1200 (the game's own mutation chance) it's blue. Caught in a Water Transport Tank with an Insect Ration (a bucket of tropical fish tempts them, but isn't the bait); let go, the game picks its colour (the game's axolotl has no event per colour). Field Journal: spot one, watch one walk along the bottom under the water. The test zoo gets an Axolotl Tank (10 by 8, water 2 deep over sand with a patch of seagrass, a bank along the back, walls 3 high, a Water Filter, an Aquarium Feeder of Insect Rations). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, runs ax2 and ax3): the tank measured ready with no warnings (8 water blocks with plants, no gravel); the axolotls swam, rested and walked along the bottom; gravel under 3 columns gave the warning with its spot and cleared when sand went back; the pair mated on the second park day and two cyan young hatched, swam and grew up; the five coat events gave variants 0 to 4, a young one was a baby at scale 0.5, wild ones came out in the game's own colours; no "No enrichment equipment" with the plants (enrichment held at 70); no content-log errors. 353 tests. How: the bench tools in the shared folder's bench/ (b185: steps_a, steps_b). A new entity: a full Minecraft restart. Test plan: docs/Axolotls_test_plan.md. Next: 0.1.186 dolphins, then the rest of research/vanilla_plan.md's order (hostile mobs from 0.1.198).
- 0.1.186 (2026-11-06): dolphins (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_dolphin`, the game's dolphin as a zoo copy (its model, skin, swim animation and the swirl behind it, its sounds; a calf drawn the 0.1.172 way, the grown model at 0.65). Rules in animals/dolphinRules.js, BALANCE.dolphin; the leap in animals/dolphin.js. The dolphin pool (profile dolphin_pool: 275 blocks of water for up to five and 75 more each, the European Association for Aquatic Mammals' housing standard at a block a metre; `firstGroup` lets a profile's base cover a group). Deep and wide: the measure reads each column of water down from its top (exhibits.js, ex.dolphin) and wants a stretch 8 by 8 blocks and 4 deep (the US rule for bottlenose dolphins, 9 CFR 3.104: 7.32 m across at the narrowest and 1.83 m deep; the EAAM's 3.5 m), else comfort is capped at 60 with a warning. Air: at least half the pool's water open to the sky (nothing right over it), else capped at 60 too. Food: Fish Rations from an Aquarium Feeder; enrichment from a Floating Toy and a Hanging Food Basket of Fish Rations (one kind alone, 12 a day against the 15 they lose, ran down on the test server). Pods: at least two. Breeding: one calf, born in the water 5 park days after mating, grown in 6. Caught in a Large Water Tank (Marine Husbandry) with a Fish Ration, raw cod or raw salmon; let go, it's the game's own, grown. Field Journal: spot one, watch one leap out of the water, swim with them (one swims alongside you). The copy: movement.basic (with no movement type it froze), its swimming navigation letting it sink (six probes on the test server: only that one swam the pool's whole depth, as the game's own do), random_swim within 8 across and 3 up or down; it breathes water as well as air (the game's own dolphins drowned in the test pool, Mojang's open MCPE-37229: in water over a block deep they don't get up to breathe); and under movement.basic the game's breach never lifts it out, so the script leaps it now and then from just under the surface, an arc half a block over the water (the game's own height, measured) and back in 3 blocks on, never through a wall or out of the pool, with the game's jump and splash sounds. The test zoo gets a Dolphin Pool (18 by 16 of water 4 deep, its top a block over the ground so a flat world's bedrock stays under it: the first pool, dug 4 deep, let the game's dolphins out of the world; a raised bank, walls 3 over the water, a Water Filter, an Aquarium Feeder of Fish Rations, a Floating Toy, a Hanging Food Basket); a blueprint's seed may give its height, and the builder can float a toy (`p.toy`, fixtures.js floatToy). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, runs breach and D to H, probes): the game's leaps topped half a block over the water and none got over a wall 1 high, so no wall rule; the pool measured 16 by 16 of water 4 deep, 287 of 288 open (the feeder), no warnings; the pair mated, a calf was born and grew up; enrichment held at 100 with the toy and basket; the copies swam from the floor to the surface and leapt 5 times in 8 minutes, half a block out, none out of the pool; no content-log errors. 355 tests. - 0.1.187 (2026-11-06): bees (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_bee`, the game's bee as a zoo copy (its model, its four skins: with pollen when it carries nectar, angry, both; its flight, flowers, nectar, hive and sting; the buzz played by the client, the game playing its own in its engine; a young one drawn the 0.1.172 way, the grown model at 0.5). Rules in animals/beeRules.js, BALANCE.bee. The bee garden (profile bee_garden: 25 blocks of floor for up to three and 6 more each, roofed 4 up). Flowers are their food: the game's 29 bee flowers (its look_for_food list) counted in the flight measure's cells and beside them (a tall one once), 2 a bee and 6 at least; with enough they need no feeder, short of them a Nectar Feeder makes up the share and comfort is capped at 60. Hives: a Beehive or Bee Nest for each 3 (the game's holds 3), counted only when its front (its direction state: 0 south, 1 west, 2 north, 3 east) opens into the garden, else capped at 60; the clipboard reads their honey from the hives as it opens. An empty exhibit with a hive beside its floor is measured for bees. The copy keeps the game's own hive: a bee goes in as an entity and comes out a new one with its dynamic properties and name, so the zoo finds it again by its rid. It can't be hurt, so a hit doesn't anger it; a hive sheared or bottled with no campfire under it sends them out angry, and a bee that stings loses its stinger (the game's look) and, where the game's dies, lives on pacified and never gets angry again. Colony: at least 3. Breeding: one young bee a park day after mating, grown in one. Caught in a Small Carrier with a Nectar Ration (they follow a flower, as the game's do); let go, it's the game's own, grown by its own minecraft:ageable_grow_up. Field Journal: spot one, watch one gather nectar in a flower, spot one carrying nectar home. The type checker: each sight function the journal spreads now declares one return type (one returning {} or an object doubled the union: at the bees' 8192 tsc ran out of room after 5 minutes; now 6 seconds). The test zoo gets a Bee Garden (9 by 9 of grass under a glass roof 4 up, nine flowers and a peony, a Beehive facing in, a Water Trough). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, runs A to E): 10 flowers and the hive counted, no warnings; the three gathered, went in and out (about 20 seconds out, 2 minutes in, by day as the game's own did beside them), honey 3 after one trip and full after two, all still known to the zoo after each trip; in at night and out at dawn; a young bee born and grown, and with four the clipboard asked for a second hive; the game's sting countdown on one: it lived on stingless; the Bench player's hit left the bee calm; shears on the full hive: honeycomb dropped, honey 0, the three stung the Bench (20 health to 15), lost their stingers and lived on, and a second shear later stung nobody; no content-log errors. 356 tests. - 0.1.188 (2026-11-06): parrots (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_parrot`, the game's parrot as a zoo copy (its model, its five skins by minecraft:variant: 0 red, 1 blue, 2 green, 3 cyan, 4 grey, the wild one's colour carried over; its animations: flying, standing, sitting, dancing, the wings beating from the game's wing query where the engine gives one, else the zoo's own beat while it flies; its sounds). It takes no damage, never despawns, is never tamed, never sits on a shoulder and ignores a cookie (the care notes say why). Rules in animals/parrotRules.js, BALANCE.parrot; the game's dancing and imitating are its engine's (the entity file has no component for either), so animals/parrot.js does both. Flight: the game's own goals, plus a goal to fly to a Perch and sit on it a while (the test server: the game's own parrots and a plain copy landed on the floor of an aviary and stayed there). The parrot aviary (profile parrot_aviary: 25 blocks of floor for a pair and 8 more each, roofed 4 up, 2 Perches or more; from the AFA's flight rule of thumb for macaws). Dancing: the jukeboxes beside the aviary's air are counted with the flight measure; while one plays (its record_player), each parrot within 3 blocks of it (the game's radius) dances (the copy's zoocraft:dance event: its flying goals off, a property the client draws as the game's dance), and stops when the track ends; a dance is music enrichment that park day and the next. Imitating: now and then a parrot near a monster within 20 blocks (the game's own or the Mob House's copy) makes its sound with the game's own pitch and volume (39 mobs from Mojang's sounds.json parrot entries), and once in a while, not on Peaceful, a monster's that isn't there. Food: seeds or Fruit Rations; a Hanging Food Basket and a Puzzle Feeder for enrichment. A flock: at least 2. Breeding: 1 or 2 eggs in a Nest Box, hatched in 3 park days, grown in 8; a chick drawn as a small grown one (0.6), in a parent's colour. Caught in a Small Carrier with seeds; let go, it's the game's own in a colour the game picks. Field Journal: spot one, watch them dance to a jukebox, hear one imitate a monster. The test zoo gets a Parrot Aviary (7 by 7 under a glass roof 4 up, Perches at three heights, a jukebox, a Nest Box, a Basic Feeder, a Puzzle Feeder and a Hanging Food Basket of Fruit Rations, a Water Trough). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, probes 1 and 2, run A): 1 jukebox and 3 Perches counted, 42 blocks usable, no warnings; in nearly 3 minutes of once-a-second looks the three were in the air 51 in 100, on a Perch, a log or the jukebox 15 and on the floor 33; with the cat disc playing all three danced by the next look (10 seconds) and stopped within 5 seconds of it stopping, the music day noted; with a zombie 7 blocks from the aviary they imitated about 10 times in 2 minutes (2 in the 3 minutes before, with no monster near); a pair mated, laid an egg in the Nest Box and it hatched a chick, small and flying; no content-log errors. 357 tests. - 0.1.189 (2026-11-07): ocelots and cats (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_ocelot` and `zoocraft:zoo_cat`, the game's ocelot and cat as zoo copies (their models; the ocelot's spotted coat, the cat's eleven by minecraft:variant, the stray's coat carried over; walk, sneak, sprint, sit and lie-down animations, the copies working out the engine's state variables for themselves; their sounds, the cat's classic or royal voice by minecraft:sound_variant, a purr while it naps). They take no damage, never despawn, are never tamed or leashed and don't hunt. Rules in animals/catRules.js and cats.js, BALANCE.cat. Shy: a caught one runs from players (the game's avoid goal) until one feeds it raw cod or salmon by hand (the game's trusting, one time in three); the zoo copy's zoocraft:on_trust keeps it on the record, so it trusts again wherever it's put, the player is told, and a kitten born in the zoo trusts from the start. Naps: ocelots by day, cats round the clock (the game's nap goal on timers). Resting spots: an ocelot rests up on a log (one each: the copy's own goal); a cat sits on a chest, the foot of a bed or a lit furnace (one for each 2), measured with the walking measure, short of them comfort capped at 55; the cat's goal sits it at a seat's edge, so the script moves it onto the middle (Bedrock's chest is 0.95 high), one by a taken seat naps there instead, and since the goal never takes one to a bed (four ways tried) a cat by a free bed hops onto its foot now and then. The ocelot exhibit (profile ocelot: 30 blocks for one or a pair and 12 more each, 4 high, cover over three tenths; SDZWA and ADW) and the Cat House (profile cat_house: 12 blocks for a pair and 4 more each, 3 high, a roof over a quarter, up to 6; the Association of Shelter Veterinarians). Breeding: ocelots 1 or 2 kittens, cats 1 to 3, drawn as small grown ones (the 0.1.172 way). Caught in a Small Carrier with raw cod or salmon (or a Meat or Fish Ration); let go, each is the game's own, a cat keeping its voice (its coat is the game's pick: the Script API's variant is read-only). Field Journal: ocelot spot one, find one napping, spot one that trusts people; cat spot one, find one curled up asleep, see one sitting up on a seat. The test zoo gets the Ocelots (8 by 8 of grass, a jungle tree's leaves for cover, a lying log and a standing one, a Scent Post, a Puzzle Feeder, a Basic Feeder and a Hanging Food Basket, a Water Trough) and a Cat House (7 by 6, a plank roof over the back corner, a bed, a chest, a Climbing Frame, feeders, a Water Trough). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, probes 1 and 2, runs A to F): 3 logs and 2 seats counted, no warnings; by day the ocelots asleep in 291 and 296 of 300 once-a-second looks, at night up in all 240; shy ones went to the far corners of the Ocelots with the Bench player in the middle, and kept 9 to 11 blocks off in the open; the first raw cod won an ocelot's trust (the message, the property, the record), a cat's after two or three, and both still trusted after being put down again, a royal voice kept; cats sat on the middle of a chest or a lit furnace and napped there, one by a taken seat lay down beside it, one hopped onto the bed's foot, sat half a minute and slept on it; a creeper shut in the Ocelots moved off from 2.6 blocks to 4 to 6; no content-log errors. 358 tests. - 0.1.192 (2026-11-07): sniffers (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_sniffer`, the game's sniffer as a zoo copy (the game's model, skin, animations and animation controllers by their own names, its sounds; snifflets the grown model at the server's 0.45 with the game's own unused baby controller, the 0.1.172 way: the game's snifflet model has another skeleton). Its digging is the game's own, kept whole and renamed zoocraft: (the scenting timer, every 400 to 500 s a 2 s long sniff; the random_search_and_dig goal, 20 blocks round, 8 to 10 s of digging, the game's loot table: a torchflower seed or a pitcher pod; standing up; the happy wiggle), the grass block named grass_block (the game's list says minecraft:grass, its old name); a grown one only goes on to dig (the game's filter). A property, zoocraft:sniffer_act (idle, sniffing, searching, digging, rising), set by those events, tells the journal what it's doing; the goal fires on_item_found, on_success, then on_searching_start, on_fail_during_searching and on_item_found again in one tick, so on_item_found says "rising". Harmless, persistent, an ambient sound interval (a zoocraft: entity needs one), no leash, balloon, breeding by seeds, eggs or healing. Rules in animals/snifferRules.js. A paddock of their own (profile medium_terrestrial): ground they dig under half the floor or more (dirt, coarse dirt, grass, podzol, rooted dirt, moss, pale moss, mud, muddy mangrove roots), a new measured kind (data/blocks.js DIG_PATTERNS, the reader's dig flag, exhibits/geometry.js countDig, ex.digCells); short of it comfort capped at 55 (BALANCE.sniffer).
