Caught in a Livestock Crate with torchflower seeds or a Grain Ration; let go by the game's spawn_adult, grown. Field Journal: spot one, watch one sniff out a spot (seedsniff), watch one dig up a seed (digup). The test zoo gets the Sniffers beside the Armadillos (12 by 12: grass, a moss bed, a dirt and coarse-dirt strip; a Basic Feeder and a Hanging Food Basket of Grain Rations, a Water Trough; a pair). The Field Journal through glass (every species): the game's view raycast stops at the first block, glass too, so an animal behind an exhibit's glass front was never counted; when the raycast and the Clear Lava walk find nothing, discovery.js throughGlass walks the line of sight block by block up to the first that hides (data/blocks.js blocksView) and takes the first animal within a block of it. Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, runs A to D): 142 of the 143 floor blocks counted (72 needed), no warnings; each of five sniffers had its first long sniff 430 to 495 s after it came and the pair dug up a seed each on their own; sent looking by the event the pair and a probe on grass blocks only dug up seeds (torchflower seeds and pitcher pods on the floor, now and then one thrown over the wall), probes on sand and on mycelium sniffed but never searched, a probe made young never searched; let go by the crate's spawn, 3 came out grown (and dug on their own); the journal ticked nothing from the path at the glass and all three tasks inside (run C), and all three from the path with throughGlass (run D); a clean content log. Not on the server: the look (it draws nothing). 361 tests. Test plan docs/Sniffers_test_plan.md.
- 0.1.193 (2026-11-07): llamas, trader llamas and mules (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_llama` and `zoocraft:zoo_trader_llama`, the game's llama and trader llama as zoo copies (the game's grown model, coats by minecraft:variant, its walk and sounds; crias the grown model at the server's 0.5 with the game's own unused baby controller, the 0.1.172 way; the chest bones hidden). Carpets: a property, zoocraft:decor (0 none, then the game's 16 in its order), drawn as the game's second texture (a trader llama with none wears its trader's blanket); the copy's own interactions, one for each carpet and shears, set it; animals/llama.js keeps it on the record (a carpet over another hands the first back, the test server: it was lost), shears give the carpet back. As the game's: a llama spits at a wild wolf within 10 blocks it can walk to (the game's targeting and ranged attack, renamed); harmless, so a stray spit does nothing to a herd mate. `zoocraft:zoo_mule`, the zoo donkey's files with the mule's skin at the game's 0.92. Rules in animals/llamaRules.js. The Paddock: llama_paddock and mule_paddock (150 blocks for the first, 50 for each more, 4 high), grass (20 each, up to half their food), a stable corner (hay 1 for every 2 llamas, 1 for each mule), a Hay Rack and a Hanging Food Basket of Grain Rations; mules gallop and are groomed as donkeys; a petting farm with a keeper. Company (species.herdWith): llamas and trader llamas count as each other's herd; horses, donkeys and mules as each other's (a lone horse with a lone donkey each lacked company before, so the pair a mule needs could never breed: the test server). Breeding: one cria each; mules have no foals (species.sterile); a horse and a donkey of opposite sexes sharing a paddock, with no pair of its own kind, have a mule foal (species.crossBreed, animals/breeding.js crossPair). Catching (Livestock Crate): llamas with a hay bale, wheat or a Grain Ration; trader llamas refused on their trader's lead (shears cut it, and the game tames one off it), caught off it; the crate's first try on one says to take off a carpet (the game puts one on a trader llama only in its inventory screen, and a script can't read that slot: no equippable component, hasitem finds nothing there even after replaceitem filled it, its inventory holds none), the next within a minute catches it, in its trader's blanket; one carrying a chest is someone's; mules (bred ones are untamed) with wheat or a Grain Ration. The crate's ride fix (main.js crateMounts) covers llamas. Let go by the game's spawn_adult, grown (a llama's coat is the game's new pick), a zoo carpet back to the player. Field Journal: llama spot, herd, carpet (a zoo one); trader llama spot, herd, onlead; mule spot, graze, herd. The journal's move gate (every species): tasks only a zoo one can show (dance, imitate, trust, sit, seedsniff, digup, carpet: TASKS[t].zoo) no longer count toward moving one (discovery/rules.js wildTasks), so wild parrots (0.1.188), ocelots and cats (0.1.189) and sniffers (0.1.192), never catchable since, can be caught; the page marks them "(a zoo one)". The test zoo gets the Llamas, Trader Llamas and Mules beside the Horses (18 by 12 each). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, runs A to F): 215 floor blocks each, 212 or 213 grass, the hay they need, an 18-block run, no warnings; carpets dressed and swapped a zoo llama (the first back), the same again did nothing, shears gave it back, a trader llama made again from its record wore its carpet; a wolf in the Llamas' paddock kept 11 to 12 blocks off, then a llama spat at it every 4 s (1 damage a hit; one spit hit the other llama, which took none); a trader llama on its trader's lead was refused and ticked "onlead", unleashed it was tamed, warned, then caught in its blanket; wild llamas caught with a hay bale and wheat in their coats, mules with wheat and a Grain Ration; a mare and a jack in the Horses' paddock (the shared-room factor eased on the bench: 287 of 325 blocks) had a mule foal, Sarge, a young zoo mule with both parents; 10 of each let go by the crate's spawn came out grown; a clean content log. Not on the server: the look (it draws nothing). 362 tests. Test plan docs/Llamas_test_plan.md.
- 0.1.194 (2026-11-07): bats and allays (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_bat` and `zoocraft:zoo_allay`, the game's bat and allay as zoo copies (the game's models, skins, animations and sounds; a bat pup is the grown model at 0.6, the 0.1.172 way: the game has no young of either). Bats (animals/bat.js, batRules.js): the game flies the copy (its float_wander); the game's bat hangs by its engine, which a copy doesn't get, so the zoo hangs it: one that settles flies up to a free roost of its house (an open block under solid roof: not glass, leaves or slabs), hangs there by the copy's zoocraft:roost property (the client's resting pose, its top against the block as the game's), turns about now and then, and flies off when someone who isn't sneaking comes within 4 blocks or its block goes. In a dark house (the nocturnal house's test) they fly and hang in bouts at any hour; in a bright one they hang all day and comfort can't rise above 55. The Bat House (bat_house): 40 blocks of floor for the first 3 and 4 more each, 4 high, a roost for each, a straight 10 blocks of flight (8 wingspans, the AZA Bat TAG); a colony of 3 to 12; Fruit Rations, berries or Insect Rations; one pup after 4 park days, grown in 6. Allays (animals/allay.js, allayRules.js): dancing is the parrots' (within 10 blocks of a jukebox that's playing); an amethyst shard on a dancing one splits it in two (the copy's own interaction, its event zoocraft:duplicate: a new zoo allay with it as parent, the game's chime), both resting 5 minutes, kept in real time (the world's time stands still with the daylight cycle off: the test server); no sexes, no breeding switch. Given an item it holds it (zoocraft:holding, set by the copy's give and take interactions: a script can't read its hand, no equippable, an empty inventory, hasitem 0) and picks up more of the same; the game's brings them to the player by its engine, the copy keeps them. The game's vibration listener is the game's allay's and warden's only (the content log: "only supported on the Warden and Allay"), so the copy goes without: a note block is enrichment. The Allay Room (allay_room): 25 blocks for the first 2 and 6 more each, 4 high; a jukebox; alone or up to 8; Fruit Rations or berries (a game choice: the game's eat nothing). Catching (Small Carrier): bats after the journal's spot, hang and colony; allays from outpost cages and mansion cells, the crate refusing one that holds an item until it's taken back with an empty hand. Field Journal: bat spot, hang, colony; allay spot, dance (a zoo one), carry (a zoo one). The test zoo gets a Bat House (stone, tinted glass, Nocturnal Lamps) and an Allay Room. Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, runs A to G): the Bat House 46 blocks of floor, 50 roosts, a 10-block flight, dark (no daylight, the brightest light 6), no warnings; zoo bats flew up and hung at the game's wild bats' height, flew off from a player walking within 4 and stayed for one sneaking; the journal's three from the zoo's bats, a wild bat refused before them and caught after; a pup, Belfry, born at 0.6; the Allay Room 32 blocks, one note block; dancing on and off with the jukebox; Chime split, Pixie new with Chime its parent, both resting 5:00 counting down; given a diamond the crate refused it, an empty hand took it back, then the crate took it; the game's allay brought a dropped diamond back to the player, the copy kept it; a wild allay warned, caught and living in the room; clean content logs after the listener came off. Not on the server: the look (it draws nothing), and a crate used on an allay by a real player (the bench's can't fire the event). 363 tests. Test plan docs/Bats_and_allays_test_plan.md.
- 0.1.195 (2026-11-07): iron, snow and copper golems (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:zoo_iron_golem`, `zoocraft:zoo_snow_golem` and `zoocraft:zoo_copper_golem`, the game's three as zoo copies (the game's models, skins, animations and sounds). Built, not born (species.built: no sexes, no young, a breeding check that says to build another and catch it); they drink nothing (species.drinksNothing: no trough, no Water Filter); food is the zoo's (iron nuggets or ingots, snowballs or snow blocks, copper nuggets or ingots: a game choice, the game's eat nothing). Iron golems (Iron Golem Yard, iron_golem_yard: 64 blocks for the first and 32 more each, 4 high): the game's targeting of monsters (not creepers) and its player-built hurt_by_target (never a player), never the zoo's own (every target filter leaves zoocraft_animal alone); village paving under a quarter of the floor or more (the mobs' floor measure, the game's preferred_path blocks), alone or up to 4. Snow golems (Snow Golem Yard, snow_golem_yard: 16 and 8 more, 3 high): their trail and snowballs (not at the zoo's own); a roof over all of it (cover 1) and a biome no warmer than 1.0 (deserts, badlands, savannas, the Nether: ex.warmBiome, comfort capped at 55 with a warning; the zoo's don't melt); the game's shears and freezing_immune left out (the pumpkin is hidden by the engine; the component isn't open to add-ons). Copper golems (Copper Golem Yard, copper_golem_yard: 16 and 8 more; animals/copperGolem.js, golemRules.js): the game's transport_items (16 blocks across), take_flower, oxidising, waxing and scraping on the zoo's own properties (zoocraft:oxidation, waxed, chest_interaction, has_flower); the statue is the zoo's (the game's is a block): fully oxidized and unwaxed, in 4 to 13 s it stands still in one of the game's four poses (zoocraft:statue, zoocraft:pose; no goals), drops what it holds and its poppy; an empty hand turns its pose, an axe scrapes a stage, and an unoxidized statue comes back to life with the next scrape. The record follows its stage, wax, statue and pose each second near a player (a respawn and a crate keep them). A copper chest and a chest measured on or beside the floor: enrichment (sorting). Catching: Reinforced Crate (iron ingot or nuggets), Livestock Crate (snowball or snow), Small Carrier (copper ingot or nuggets; refuses a statue; warns once for one carrying an item). Released as the game's: an iron golem as one a player built, a copper golem at its stage, waxed again. Field Journal: spot; iron rounds, snow trail, copper at a chest. Golem Yard (community golems): the three may share. The test zoo gets the three yards (walls 3 high: a golem up on a chest or a feeder steps over 2). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, runs A to F): first the snow golem wouldn't load (freezing_immune "not present in the Schema"; its ranged attack written for 1.26.50, so each copy now keeps the game's own format, never below 1.26.10) and the copper golems walked out over a chest against a wall 2 high; then all three yards ready with no warnings (the iron yard 80 blocks, 32 paved; the snow yard 29 roofed, cool; the copper yard 1 copper chest and 2 chests); the iron golem killed a wild zombie in 4 s; the snow golems' snowballs hit one; copper golems put 16 cobblestone in one chest and 4 poppies in the other (with one chest the poppies stayed in a hand: the game's chest rule); the snow trail; a copper golem set weathered (its record followed), waxed with a honeycomb, unwaxed and scraped a stage with an axe, put down again weathered and waxed; turned to a statue in about 10 s with its poppy dropped, its pose turned twice, put down again a statue, refused by the carrier, scraped back to copper and back to life; the carrier warned once for one carrying, then took it; the game's copper golem made weathered and waxed, caught and living in the yard both still; the game's iron golem (built) caught and living in the yard; the game's snow golem caught; breeding on said they aren't born; a Snow Golem Yard in a desert flat world measured warm, warned, no trail, comfort falling toward 55, the golems unharmed; the journal's rounds (the iron golem walked 31 of 60 s), trail and chest; clean content logs. Never bred; a pen each. Fix: the zoo sulfur cube (0.1.197) never squished or made its hop sounds (the engine keeps its squish for its own cubes); its client works it out the same way now. Test zoo: Slimes (mud, a clay patch) and Magma Cubes (basalt, a blackstone patch, walls 6 high) Mob House pens, a small, a medium and a large one in each. Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, plains flat worlds, runs P, A, B, C and D): the copies rose as high as the game's at every size and hopped about as often; the engine's squish value changed on every one of the game's slimes, magma cubes and sulfur cubes and never on a copy; both pens ready (floors 99%), no warnings, the six at variant 1, 2 and 4 with 1, 4 and 16 health; the journal from zoo ones; the floors made dirt gave the floor warnings (21%); breeding refused; a strong large slime shook the serum off, calmed at 4 of 16 (large, 4 of 16), a medium one refused at 2 of 4 and calmed at 1, a small one calmed as it was; the cage refused before the journal and without a slimeball, then caught; each came out in its pen at its size (coats 2, 1, 0); a large and a small magma cube caught with magma cream, refused in the Slimes' pen; a calmed large slime and magma cube killed left nothing (the game's split in two); clean content logs. Not on the server: the looks, the squish and the sounds (the client's). 373 tests. Test plan docs/Slimes_and_magma_cubes_test_plan.md.
- 0.1.204 (2026-11-07): the witch (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md; the phantom moves to 0.1.205: the game's own circling sat at a pen's roof on the test server, so it needs scripted flight). `zoocraft:calm_witch`, `zoocraft:zoo_witch`: the zoo zombie's entities with the witch's family, 26 health, box and breath, the speed scaled as the zombie's (0.17, 0.2), Grain Rations tempting it; no potions thrown or drunk, no attack, climbing, raids, loot or despawning; a player's hits alone hurt a calmed one, nothing hurts a zoo one. The game's client (format 1.10.0, its controllers in animations + animate) and the game's purple sparkles over its hat now and then from the client (`controller.animation.zoocraft.witch_spell`, 20 to 110 seconds apart, the game's particle as `zoocraft:witch_spell`); its sounds by script (ambient and hurt). Pen (mob_pen): a swamp hut's floor or swamp ground (spruce planks, mud, muddy mangrove roots or clay) under half the floor and a cauldron in the pen, or comfort can't rise above 55; no dark; Grain Rations from a Basic Feeder. Never bred; a pen of its own. Test zoo: Witches (spruce planks, a mud patch, a cauldron, glass to the hall), a pair. Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, plains flat worlds, runs P, A, B and C): the game's phantom and copies with its goals sat at a 13-wide box's roof; the Witches pen ready (floor 97%, the cauldron counted), no warnings; the floor made dirt and the cauldron taken out gave their warnings and cleared once put back; breeding refused; a strong witch shook the serum off; one weakened to 6 drank and was at 10 three seconds later (refused); weakened again and calmed at once at 6 of 26; a calmed one never drank (6 of 26 ten seconds on) and followed a Grain Ration; the cage refused before the journal and without a Grain Ration, then caught; each came out in its pen; clean content logs. - 0.1.205 (2026-11-07): the phantom (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md), flown by script. `zoocraft:calm_phantom`, `zoocraft:zoo_phantom`: the zoo zombie's entities with the phantom's family, 20 health, 0.9 by 0.5 box and breath (air and water), and the Void Rays' flying parts (no gravity, collisions, hover navigation and movement, no speed, no pushing) and no goals: no attack, swoop, circling, cat-avoiding, burning, loot or despawning; a player's hits alone hurt a calmed one, nothing hurts a zoo one. The game's own circling sits a phantom at a pen's roof (0.1.204), so `animals/mobFlight.js` flies them every tick near players (within 48 blocks) with the Void Rays' glide maths and pushes (`animals/mobFlightRules.js`, pure): a zoo one circles a ring under its pen's roof (1.4 in from the walls, 1.6 under the roof, three lanes: a narrower one a block lower the other way round), slower where the ring bends tight, and every 10 to 20 seconds (the game's Bedrock phantom's) swoops down to 1.3 over the floor for 2.5 seconds; a calmed one circles slowly (2 blocks a second) 2 blocks over the ground where it was calmed (one calmed high up comes down to find the ground, 64 blocks searched), and at full speed over anyone within 10 blocks holding a Meat Ration. The pen measure keeps the box of the pen's air (`ex.mobPen.air`) for the rings. Caught the zombie's way (Mob Taming; a quarter of its health, 5 of 20, its own words when it shakes the serum off: hit it as it swoops; a Mob Cage with a Meat Ration; the serum's words name it). The game's client (its wings, tail, pitch and wing smoke) with the flap sound named for the copies; its calls by script (idle and hurt). Pen (mob_pen_sky: 6 x 6 of floor for the first, 12 more each, 6 blocks of headroom): the dark, Meat Rations from a Basic Feeder. Never bred; a pen of its own. Data checks: a flown mob lives in mob_pen_sky (and only it), a mob of the dark, its kinds flown. Test zoo: Phantoms (11 by 13, walls 8 high, tinted glass, a lamp hung from the middle of the roof), a pair; 11 wide keeps the test zoo's ring at 250. Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, plains flat worlds, runs A and C): the pen ready, dark, no warnings; both phantoms flown at 4.4 blocks a second for a minute, always inside the pen's air (1.6 or more from a wall, 0.9 under the roof), 2 or 3 swoops in 40 seconds, never still; the journal filled through the tinted glass; a strong one shook the serum off; a weakened one calmed at 5 of 20 and circled 2 over the ground; one calmed 40 blocks up came down to 2 over the ground; the cage refused before the journal and without a Meat Ration, then caught, and the caught one flew in its pen; a calmed one followed a Meat Ration 12 blocks and circled where it was left; clean content logs. Not on the server: the looks and the sounds (the client's). 375 tests. Test plan docs/Phantoms_test_plan.md.
- 0.1.206 (2026-11-07): the piglin, the piglin brute and the zombified piglin (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:calm_piglin`, `zoocraft:zoo_piglin`, `zoocraft:calm_piglin_brute`, `zoocraft:zoo_piglin_brute`, `zoocraft:calm_zombified_piglin`, `zoocraft:zoo_zombified_piglin`: the zoo zombie's entities with each one's family, health (16, 50, 20), box, breath (piglins and brutes air only, zombified piglins water too) and speed (scaled from the game's 0.35 and 0.23), zombified piglins fire immune; no attack, shooting, zombifying, shaking, hunting, picking up, loot or despawning. Each keeps the weapon it held (the record's coat: a piglin's golden sword, crossbow or golden spear, a zombified piglin's golden sword, golden spear, crossbow or golden axe, a brute's golden axe; `zoocraft:coat_N` adds `zoocraft:gear_N`, minecraft:variant N and the zoo's one-item equipment table, never dropped) and a piglin's or zombified piglin's golden armour (the record's `gold`, a mask: 1 helmet, 2 chestplate, 4 leggings, 8 boots). A mob has no equippable component for a script (the test server), so `animals/gold.js` reads the hand and armour with the game's hasitem test run as the mob and puts armour on with replaceitem (`animals/mobRules.js` GOLD_ARMOR, goldMask, goldCommands, goldWords, hasItemCommand; lookEvents takes the held item). A piglin barters as the game's (inventory, admire_item, barter with the game's own piglin_barter table, the admire and barter goals, the interaction off while it looks an ingot over); brutes and zombified piglins never take gold. The game's clients (the grown model alone for piglins and zombified piglins, their own render controllers; attachables on), the admiring sound named for the copies, their calls by script; a parrot imitates a zoo zombified piglin. Caught the zombie's way (Mob Taming; a quarter of their health, 4 of 16, 12 of 50, 5 of 20, each with its own words when it shakes the serum off; a Mob Cage with a Meat Ration). Pens (mob_pen): piglins on a bastion's or crimson forest's ground with a block of gold or more, brutes on a bastion's floor, zombified piglins on the Nether wastes' ground; Meat Rations. Piglins and brutes share a pen (the Bastion); never bred. Data checks: a weapon a coat (the calmed one's own), armour only with a weapon kept, bartering the piglin's alone. Test zoo: three 11 by 11 Mob Houses (Piglins with a block of gold), a pair each; the test zoo's ring may now reach 300 (was 250). Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, plains flat worlds, runs A to D): the three pens ready with no warnings, every mob holding its coat's weapon; the gold warning without the block and gone with it; a zoo piglin, a calmed one and the game's own bartered (soul sand, a fire charge, blackstone into the player's inventory; the ingot used up in Survival, a second one while it looked one over taken but no second trade), brutes and zombified piglins never; strong ones refused with their own words; a piglin with a golden helmet and boots calmed at 4 of 16, caught after the journal and with a Meat Ration, and in the zoo with the same sword, helmet and boots; a brute calmed at 12 of 50 (13 refused) and put in the Piglins pen; a piglin given a crossbow and golden leggings turned in about 15 seconds, calmed at 5 of 20 with both, caught and in the zoo with both; a calmed zombified piglin refused by the Piglins pen; a baby refused; clean content logs. Not on the server: the looks and the sounds (the client's). 376 tests. Test plan docs/Piglins_test_plan.md.

- 0.1.208 (2026-11-07): the blaze and the ghast (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:calm_blaze`, `zoocraft:zoo_blaze`, `zoocraft:calm_ghast`, `zoocraft:zoo_ghast`: the zoo zombie's entities with each one's family, health (20, 10), box (0.5 by 1.8, 4.02 by 4), breath (the blaze the game's default, the ghast the game's) and speed (scaled from the game's 0.23 and 0.03), both fire immune; no fireballs, flames, attack, loot or despawning. The blaze walks (the game's does with no target); the ghast floats by the game's own float_wander and float navigation with no gravity, its walking taken out, and a float_tempt brings it down to a Meat Ration held up (at the zoo's format 1.26.50 a float_wander's float_duration is an object {min, max}: the game's 1.26.0 file writes a list, which the test server refused with "float_duration: expected an object"). The game's clients (the blaze by the game's render controller, its 1.8.0 move controller now scripts.animate at 1.10.0, no charged flames; the ghast by its own render controller with the plain skin, no scale animation), their calls by script; a parrot imitates them. Caught the zombie's way (Mob Taming; a quarter of their health, 5 of 20 and 2.5 of 10, each with its own words when it shakes the serum off; a Mob Cage with an Ember Ration for a blaze, a Meat Ration for a ghast). Pens: blazes (mob_pen) on a Nether fortress's floor (nether bricks), Ember Rations, a Lava Trough to drink from; ghasts in the new ghast pen (mob_pen_ghast: 16 by 16 for the first, 96 more blocks for each more, 12 of headroom) over the Nether's ground, Meat Rations; a data check that a floating mob is the ghast, in its pen; never together. The carrier asks for its bait by the ration's own name (`rationOne()`: "You need an Ember Ration", not "a ember ration"). The pen measure keeps an emptied mob pen's record: it is decided by the mobs living there when the measure starts as well as when it reaches the pen (a measure started while a ghast lived there, the ghast caught out before the job reached the pen: the start's lead species said "not empty", the live residents "no mobs", so the pen went unmeasured and every mob after was refused until the pen was marked again; since 0.1.163). Test zoo: an 11 by 11 Mob House for two blazes and a 17 by 20 one, 12 high, for a ghast; the zoo packs into a 262 ring now. Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, plains flat worlds, runs A and C): both pens ready with no warnings, the ghast's 12 high; over 13 readings the ghast floated from the floor to 8 up, always inside its pen, and the blazes walked their floor; the journal filled from the hall; neither breeds; strong ones refused with their own words; a blaze calmed at 5 of 20 (6 refused), caught after the journal and only with an Ember Ration, and in the zoo; a ghast calmed at 2 of 10 (3 refused), caught with a Meat Ration, and floating in the zoo; a calmed ghast came to a held Meat Ration; a calmed blaze refused by the Ghasts pen; a measure started and the ghast taken out in the same tick left the pen with no record and the next ghast refused before the fix, and with it the record kept and the next ghast caught and moved in; clean content logs. Not on the server: the looks and the sounds (the client's). 380 tests. Test plan docs/Blazes_test_plan.md.
- 0.1.209 (2026-11-07): the enderman and the shulker (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:calm_enderman`, `zoocraft:zoo_enderman`, `zoocraft:calm_shulker`, `zoocraft:zoo_shulker`: the zoo zombie's entities with each one's family, health (40, 30), box (0.6 by 2.9, 1 by 1), breath (the game's) and speed (the enderman's scaled from the game's 0.3, its full-block step; the shulker 0: it never moves, has no stroll, tempt or homing, is pushed only by pistons and you can stand on it), the shulker fire immune; no attack, anger, teleport component, block taking, bullets, loot or despawning. Both from the End, on Chorus Browse, a Water Trough, in a Mob House pen (mob_pen). A zoo enderman blinks about its pen by script (animals/ender.js, enderRules.js): every 20 to 45 s to a land cell of its pen's last measure 3 to 10 blocks off and 3 up or down at most, with three open blocks over solid ground, the portal particles and the game's teleport sound at both ends; never out of its pen, never while a player within 6 blocks holds a Mob Cage or Chorus Browse, never the calmed one. A shulker keeps its colour (MOB_LOOKS coats 17: the coat groups set the game's minecraft:variant, 16 undyed; the release's coat list too; species seedCoats picks the test zoo's pair) and can't be led (MOB_INFO stays: the serum's words end "It never moves on its own."). The game's clients at 1.10.0 (the enderman by the game's render controller with its look, move, bob and base pose; the shulker by its render controller, facing, move and look animations, its peek driven in its own pre_animation from a small controller: every 4 to 14 s a peek of 1 to 3 s at the game's 0.3, with the game's open and close sounds), their calls by script. Caught the zombie's way (Mob Taming; a quarter of their health, 10 of 40 and 7.5 of 30, each with its own words when it shakes the serum off; a Mob Cage with Chorus Browse). Floors: endermen the End's ground (end stone, end stone bricks or obsidian), shulkers an End city's (purpur blocks, purpur pillars or end stone bricks); never together. A species' plural (`plural`, "Endermen") now reaches the room report, the clipboard and the conservation notes. Test zoo: two 11 by 11 Mob Houses (end stone with an obsidian patch; purpur with an end stone brick patch, an undyed and a lime shulker); the zoo packs into a 263 ring now. Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, plains flat worlds, runs A and C): both pens ready with no warnings, each floor 99% its own blocks; 6 blinks in 120 s, all to open land cells of the pen 3.2 to 10 blocks off, the endermen inside their pen in every reading; 0 blinks in 60 s with a Mob Cage held in the pen, 3 in the next 60 s empty-handed; the shulkers never moved and kept their colours; the journal filled from the hall; neither breeds; strong ones refused with their own words; an enderman calmed at 10 of 40 (11 refused), caught after the journal and only with Chorus Browse, and in the zoo; a lime shulker calmed at 7.5 of 30 (8 refused) still lime, caught and lime in the zoo; an undyed one calmed undyed; a calmed enderman came to held Chorus Browse (12 blocks to 2 in 10 s); a calmed enderman refused by the Shulkers pen; clean content logs. Not on the server: the looks, the peeking and the sounds (the client's). 382 tests. Test plan docs/Endermen_test_plan.md.
- 0.1.210 (2026-11-07): the pillager, the vindicator, the evoker and the vex (module 12, the plan's order in research/vanilla_plan.md). `zoocraft:calm_`/`zoo_` pillager, vindicator, evoker and vex: the zoo zombie's entities with each one's family, health (24, 24, 24, 14), box, breath and speed (the illagers' 0.35 and the evoker's 0.5 with its 0.6 stroll, scaled from the zombie's), the vex fire immune; no attack, crossbow shots, fangs, vexes, sheep turning, raids, loot or despawning. Each keeps the game's gear from the zoo's own tables, never dropped: the pillager its crossbow, the vindicator its iron axe (stowed by the game's render controller: arms crossed), the vex its iron sword; the evoker none. A captain keeps its ominous banner (MOB_LOOKS coats 2 with `captain`: the wild one's minecraft:variant, 1 a captain; gear groups set the game's variant and equipment, the captain's the banner too, in the chest slot as the game's; the release's coat list too). The vex hovers (`mobHovers`: no gravity, but it has collision, so the pen holds it): the zoo one drifts about its pen (random_hover 1 to 2 blocks up, written as the game's happy ghast writes it at our format: whole blocks in a {min, max} object; the bee's list form and a min of 0.5 fail to load at 1.26.50), the calmed one has no random goal, as the game's vex: it hangs where it was calmed until a Grain Ration tempts it (vertically too). All four Overworld mobs on Grain Rations, a Water Trough, in a Mob House pen (mob_pen), on an outpost's or a woodland mansion's floor (dark oak planks, birch planks or cobblestone), sharing a pen as the Illager Camp (exhibits/mixing.js). Caught the zombie's way (Mob Taming; a quarter of their health, 6 of 24 and 3.5 of 14, each with its own words when it shakes the serum off; a Mob Cage with a Grain Ration). The game's clients at 1.10.0, no spawn egg (the vex's 1.8.0 controllers as scripts.animate: its look and idle); calls by script. Test zoo: four 11 by 11 Mob Houses (dark oak with a cobblestone patch, a pillager and a captain; dark oak with a birch patch, a vindicator and a captain; birch with a dark oak patch, two evokers; cobblestone with a dark oak patch, two vexes); the zoo packs into a 264 ring now. Test server (BDS 1.26.62.1, plains flat worlds, runs A, C, V, W and X): all four pens ready with no warnings, each floor 99% its own blocks; each zoo pillager a crossbow, vindicator an iron axe, vex an iron sword, the captains the banner too (variant 1); the zoo vexes inside their pen in every reading over two minutes, between the floor and the roof; the journal filled from the hall (the pillagers' once a guest was out of the Bench's view); none breeds; comfort 71 to 90 in a park day; a captain refused at 24 and 7, calmed at 6 still a captain, caught after the journal and only with a Grain Ration, a captain in the zoo; a vindicator and an evoker calmed at 6, caught and in the zoo; a vex refused at 4, calmed at 3.5; a calmed vex (and one summoned calmed) never moved in 40 s, then came 7.7 blocks to a held Grain Ration; a vex caught into the Evokers pen (Evoker+Vex, no warnings); clean content logs. Not on the server: the looks and the sounds (the client's). 383 tests. Test plan docs/Pillagers_test_plan.md.
